# NEXUS — Agent Capability Gap & Roadmap I2-B → M


> **Codex handoff — 17 Oktober 2026:** status authoritative terbaru ada di **§11.16**: PARTIAL; offline certification BLOCKED; I2-B/Gate J HOLD. Klaim kesiapan dan budget §11.15 adalah historical dan telah dikoreksi. Tidak ada izin atau eksekusi live baru.

> **Living status — 17 Oktober 2026:** current authority untuk status/progression ada di **§11**. D1/S1 implemented; native I2-B tetap HOLD. D2 inconclusive; D3 ditutup dengan environmental toolchain blocker. Decoder C++ work ditutup. Generation/termination collector preflight **14/14 offline PASS**, belum ada izin inference. §1–10 di bawah dipertahankan sebagai historical audit 16 Oktober; pending review/risk/hash di sana bukan current status bila telah diperbarui di §11.

> **Latest reference study — §11.10 & §11.11:** installed Harness/OpenCode/Codex identities dan matching release-source pipelines telah dibandingkan secara read-only. §11.11 menyajikan perbandingan komprehensif Unsloth Desktop (Studio + llama.cpp b11160 Vulkan + Python backend exact release v0.1.900-beta) dengan verifikasi REA 6.5.0, 58/58 offline test suite PASS, dan pemetaan OSS Reuse First untuk tool healing, Gemma dialect handling, atomic context compaction, serta guard keamanan broken-JSON. Satu prepared native observation §11.7 tetap next action yang memerlukan approval terpisah; studi ini mengonsumsi 0 model/Agent allocation.

Tanggal: 16 Oktober 2026. Target: **native C#/.NET 8/WPF**. Status: **focused read-only audit selesai; implementasi menunggu owner review**. Iterasi kosmetik dihentikan. **I2-B native acceptance HOLD; Gate J HOLD.** Roadmap ini bukan approval memulai Gate J–M.

Primary: OpenCode, model yang dideklarasikan environment `openai/gpt-6.1-sol`; identitas tersebut bukan independent provider attestation. Research: tool MCP REA yang tersedia, server/package **6.5.0 terverifikasi melalui call aktual**. Tidak mengklaim ada separate subagent delegation yang tidak direkam. Semua call REA mendapat izin owner per-call.

## 1. Executive decision

Nexus tidak membutuhkan writer, diff engine, runtime owner, atau shell kedua. Jalur existing **exact-plan approval → SafeWrite → fixed verification → hash-guarded recovery** harus dipertahankan. Gap usability terbesarnya adalah:

1. **NL proposal belum mencapai native tool call yang diterima** pada I2-B: dua run gagal `OutputLimit`; bukan kegagalan approval atau build pada run tersebut.
2. Pemahaman proyek masih bounded static catalog + lexical fragments; belum ada model-driven repo exploration atau semantic index pada jalur Agent yang ditinjau.
3. Editing production masih dua root/profile compiled-in, dua target sempit; bukan kemampuan mengedit arbitrary approved repository secara umum.
4. Chat persistence dan metadata activity belum setara durable Agent trajectory dengan crash reconciliation/resume yang aman.
5. Registry/DI Nexus sudah menjadi titik perluasan yang layak, tetapi bukan plugin/MCP platform dinamis. Marketplace, arbitrary shell, dan multi-agent autonomy bukan prasyarat Beta.

**Prioritas:** tutup discriminator compatibility I2-B dengan replay evidence existing, verifikasi lifecycle consent C1.1 yang baru, lalu terapkan hanya perbaikan yang terbukti dan disetujui. Jangan menaikkan cap atau menambahkan parser fallback untuk memperoleh PASS. Setelah I2-B berhasil pada scope yang jujur, bangun discovery/read tools, reviewed-project onboarding, bounded coding workflow, dan durable receipts. First usable Beta dinilai melalui tiga skenario nyata di §8, bukan kesamaan tampilan.

| HISTORICAL EXECUTED | Laporan/receipt eksekusi Gate sebelumnya; bukan fresh execution sesi audit ini. |
| SOURCE VERIFIED | Source current dibaca langsung; membuktikan implementasi statis, bukan perilaku runtime baru. README ditandai sebagai documented contract bila implementasinya tidak dibaca seluruhnya. |
| REA VERIFIED — STATIC | Call REA aktual atas shipped artifact dengan Evidence ID; authority terbatas pada hasil dan coverage call tersebut. |
| INFERRED / OPEN | Kesimpulan atau risiko yang belum dibuktikan melalui reproduksi/acceptance. |

## 5. Root blockers — I2-B, separated by stage

### 5.1 Actual evidence, not another live probe

Existing evidence root: `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate-i2b` (E). Both request files were re-read structurally: model `gemma-4-e4b`, `max_tokens=256`, `stream=false`, `tool_choice=required`, `enable_thinking=false`, sole tool `nexus_apply_source_plan`.

| Run | Response fact | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| `3cc9bb20-462f-41e9-9414-dbc27f499ed0` | Reported 74.187 s; 1033 prompt /256 completion; finish `length`, tool-like **content** with `<|...|>` markers, no native tool_calls. E `ownership-recovery/native-diagnostic.json` shows continuation into source echo. | `NativeToolProtocol.Parse` rejects at line38 **before** native argument/proposal validation. Zero accepted tool call/approval/write/build/test. |
| `0020ccb4-1879-426e-a655-c1159fea8b62` | Reported 75.299 s; 1021 prompt /256 completion; finish `length`; content has fixed bool initializers and continues `File c: None` through further slots; no tool_calls. Response fingerprint `b11057-59657a613`. | Same early safe failure. No evidence that “only a little more output” would yield a valid semantic task solution. |

Budget historical **2/4 native generations, 2/2 actual Agent runs consumed**. Remaining generation slots do not authorize a third Agent run. No generations consumed here.

### 5.2 Causal separation

| Layer | Established | Still open / next discriminator |
|---|---|---|
| Model generation + stop | Actual generation hit configured 256 cap and emitted unwanted continuation. | Why it did not terminate correctly: prompt interaction, chat template, stop-token handling or provider dialect? Raw API output cannot isolate these. A cap increase is not a proven causal fix. |
| Runtime dialect/tool decoding | Content is call-like, but API `tool_calls` absent. Adapter requires standard native envelope; pinned known model/engine hash says Supported, not universal task success. | Whether current server's model template/tool parser failed to recognize generated dialect, or model failed to emit a valid dialect. Need exact engine/template/tokenizer/config evidence or controlled owner-approved test; source from another llama.cpp revision is not sufficient. |
| Nexus wire parser | Source confirms strict duplicate/shape/id/finish validation; length rejected. Existing response is not accepted. | No evidence of a host parser defect on a valid native envelope from these failures. Native parser may be tested with known-good envelopes, never “repair” call-like content with regex. |
| Proposal admission | Validator exists and deterministic cases historical PASS; live runs never reached `SourceProposalScope.Accept`. | Accepting native syntax would not establish range/semantic correctness. For second response, visible `old` adds `= 0;` where pinned B anchor lacks it; exact-original validator would require rejection if submitted as those args. This is static hypothetical classification, not an executed model proposal. |
| Task semantics | First diagnostic visible proposed `IsCpu => ... == Cpu` although controlled-failure instruction requested “provider bukan CPU”. Second uses constant/default booleans, not proven dynamic semantics. | Review/tests remain necessary even with perfect transport. Do not call truncated advisory text a complete/accepted proposal. Required facts must distinguish CPU vs non-CPU and zero vs positive count, not pass one default-state example only. |
| Approval/write/build/recovery | Existing I1 and deterministic I2-B evidence strong; raw live response rejected upstream. | No basis to patch SafeWrite/verifier or resurrect the closed SDK MIC blocker as the cause of current NL failure. Actual NL success and controlled failure still must reach these stages. |

**Root blocker conclusion:** established immediate blocker is generation ending `length` with no accepted native call. Underlying model/template/runtime decoding cause **not yet isolated**. “Increase max_tokens”, “parser bug”, “model cannot code”, and “fixed by Harness adapter” are not supported final diagnoses.

### 5.3 Directed closure, not recurring audit

1. Freeze the existing two requests/responses as immutable offline regression fixtures; preserve errors, hashes, usage/finish/has-tool metadata. Replay through existing adapter/parser and fake/no-side-effect tools. Failure is expected and must remain visibly classified. A known-good native envelope and structurally invalid proposals are separate controls.
2. Produce a single layer-by-layer outcome table and exact runtime/template identity gap. No fresh model call is needed for this fixture work. If the offline evidence cannot select a causal patch, stop with the **one missing discriminator**, not a broad re-audit.
3. Any production prompt/template/adapter change needs an exact file/diff/acceptance scope; **cap/runtime/parser/policy changes remain separately authorized**. A recorded text fragment must never become executable authority. Keep the pinned runtime until a replacement is explicitly approved.
4. Only after selected remediation + targeted safety regressions, request/consume a new finite live allocation on disposable reviewed workspace, with no retry/top-up. Required NL success: actual native call → host-valid intent → exact diff before writes → independent approval → two-file mutation → build/required facts/current hashes+MVID → honest final receipt. Controlled failure must prove recovery independently. Failures remain failures; no Option B exemption expansion.
5. At observed ~75 s before approval, remaining ≤45 s must still fit human approval and verification inside hard120 s. Measure the end-to-end remaining budget; do not pause/refill deadline. A different admission/approval lifecycle would be a separate security/behavior scope, not a timing workaround.

### 5.4 New post-H safety/functional delta: C1.1 JIT pending intent

**SOURCE OBSERVATION / OPEN RISK, not reproduced exploit:** `ConversationViewModel.cs:1107–1109,2577–2583` stores pending question/description without captured root/session/revision. `RevokeProjectRead`/`RevokeAndDrainProjectAsync:1195–1205` clear grants/context but do not visibly clear those pending fields. `ConfirmProjectReadPermissionAsync:2605–2613` grants for the **current** selection then submits the saved question. Existing grant checks do not alone prove that a consent dialog still names the same subject as the pending question after a switch.

Needed narrow directed test: request context on project/session A → switch/revoke/navigate before confirming → old pending request cannot grant or dispatch on B. Also test a switch during async grant. Minimal remediation if RED: bind pending intent to root+session+revision, invalidate on transitions, recheck before grant/resubmit; preserve opaque I2-A grant rules. This is capability safety, **not cosmetic UI work**. No claim that source leaked, a wrong grant occurred, or all C1.1 tests are invalid.

Additional non-security usability gap: `NeedsProjectContext:2592–2602` uses broad words such as “bagaimana/jelaskan” and includes extensions the reader does not support; after grant `ProjectReadEnabled=true` makes all subsequent project questions contextual. Do not advertise this heuristic as precise intent understanding or support for Python/TS/XAML reading. Fix routing only with scoped examples/acceptance, not a new model classifier.

## 6. Preserve / adapt / absent / remove-or-defer summary

- **Must KEEP:** native WPF/DI/shared runtime; Chat and AI Check; approved current C1.1 user work; I2-A opaque grants/path identity/exclusions; strict native schema; host-created proposal/profile; exact digest one-shot approval; SafeWrite/backup/reverse hash recovery; artifact provenance and required facts; private LocalLow verifier; verified anonymous Job ownership; hard120/native256 and current per-profile finite budgets.
- **ADAPT OSS first:** model-facing bounded search/read presentation and omissions; provider capability/typed finish diagnostics; task-plan representation without granting authority; durable run-event/receipt projections; stable external tool naming only when a real MCP use case is approved. Port small concepts into existing C# services, not package trees.
- **Missing or not proven usable:** iterative Agent repo exploration, broader C# project onboarding, NL-native end-to-end success, arbitrary bounded target selection within a reviewed project, durable Agent crash reconciliation, long-task evidence-backed resumability, product MCP bridge. Missing-on-reviewed-path is not a repository-wide theorem of absence.
- **REMOVE from near-term scope/claims:** cosmetic parity rounds, “100% certified” from screenshot/unit-only evidence, plan-mode-as-security, source tree size as understanding, call-like text as tool-call success, model self-report as verification. No source deletion authorized.
- **DEFER:** semantic/vector index, general LSP, parallel write calls, multi-agent teams, persistent terminal, cloud/provider replacement, browser/computer automation, plugin marketplace/self-install, PTC/workflow runtime, background unlimited loops. Reopen only with a failed real Beta requirement and explicit security/dependency review.

## 7. Dependency roadmap — proposal, not execution authorization

Gate naming below defines proposed capability scope; it does not silently revise any existing approved Gate contract. No dates determine progression.

```text
Gate H immutable baseline ── I1 + I2-A closed safety/read foundations
                                 │
              I2-B compatibility closure + C1.1 consent fence
                                 │
                J: grant-bound repo exploration/context
                                 │
       K: reviewed-project onboarding + bounded coding execution
                                 │
         L: durable trajectory, interrupted-run reconciliation
                                 │
         M: frozen usable-Beta acceptance (three real scenarios)

Optional later: MCP/LSP/parallelism — not on the mandatory Beta path
```

| Scope / priority | Deliverable dan dependency | Exit criteria / limits |
|---|---|---|
| **I2-B-D1, P0** | Implement immutable offline compatibility replay coverage using existing raw evidence; no production behavior change. Proposed new test file `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/I2BNativeCompatibilityReplayTests.cs` + owned external fixture collector/data, distinct from consumed live receipts. | Both captured responses reject as OutputLimit, zero dispatch/approval/writes; good native response reaches a fake tool only; malformed/duplicate/multi/invalid-finish stays denied; proposal byte/range/semantic test outcomes separated. No generation/engine/model install. Not equivalent to closing functional I2-B. |
| **I2-B-S1, P0 safety companion** | Directed C1.1 pending-consent RED test, then minimal fix if reproduced: `ConversationViewModel.cs` + `ConversationViewModelTests.cs` only. | Old A pending request cannot grant/read/dispatch on B after project/session/revoke/navigation; async race fenced; ordinary grant/revoke and draft/send routes retained. No new authority or dialog redesign. |
| **I2-B-D2, conditional P0** | Apply the one causal compatibility remediation identified by D1 + runtime evidence. Exact production files/budget must be reviewed before editing. | Strict safety + actual NL success/recovery at finite predeclared budget. Cannot pre-authorize parser/cap/runtime/policy edits through this roadmap. If cause still unknown, state missing discriminator and remain HOLD. |
| **Gate J, P1 — read exploration** | After I2-B exit and explicit Gate J approval: expose list/search/read over existing grant/catalog; better relevance within current limits; .cs/.csproj/.sln/.slnx first, no automatic wider extensions. | Agent can answer a question requiring ≥2 relevant files via bounded actual context; provenance/omissions visible; revoke/switch/drift/injection negatives fail closed; no shell, builds, imports evaluation, or persisted source cache. A cached index is not required. |

Replace single hard-coded pilot admission with owner-reviewed immutable profile/catalog entries and trusted verification harness, not arbitrary `.csproj` execution. Use existing plan/diff/writer. | At least one non-Alpha/Beta C# task with behavioral change across two targets, actual exact approval and verified build/tests. Success baseline revision reviewed explicitly for next task; no auto-trust/rebaseline. Unknown hooks/imports/analyzers/restore/external references rejected. Baseline admission/receipt identities persisted before side effects. |

Adapt append-only run journal/projections beside chat store; record proposed/approved/committed/verified/recovered facts, interrupted outcomes and lineage. | Restart with unresolved effect reports Interrupted/OutcomeUnknown, reconciles owned backups/hash facts, never re-executes writes or restores grants from chat. ## 8. First usable Beta — concrete acceptance

Beta means usable on **explicitly onboarded C#/.NET 8 project profiles**, not arbitrary repositories/languages, unrestricted shell, or equivalence to all Harness plugins. Freeze case inventory and model allocation before execution; no invented reliability percentage from two or three runs. Record attempted/succeeded/failed counts and all failures. Unit/replay/WPF fixture proof cannot substitute actual model-driven workflow proof.

### B1 — memahami proyek yang disetujui

- Actual WPF: pilih fresh approved project, lalu pertanyaan nyata: “Jelaskan alur request dari service A ke komponen B dan tunjukkan bagian cancellation/error handling.” Jawaban memerlukan ≥2 source files, bukan contoh path tunggal.
- Sebelum explicit read consent: zero project source reads/model source dispatch. Sesudah consent: grant-bound exploration obtains actual fragments; jawaban cites supplied ranges/hashes dan menyebut coverage gaps/external references. Human-reviewed answer agrees with the code; citation-in-range alone is insufficient semantic correctness.
- Revoke/expiry/project/session switch and pending-JIT switch negatives prevent subsequent reads and late answers in wrong scope. Unsupported/oversized project explains limit instead of claiming complete understanding. No build/index server launched by read action.

### B2 — mengusulkan dan menjalankan perubahan setelah approval

- Actual NL task on an approved disposable project, not main Nexus tree: add/adjust real behavior in two selected .cs files; property behavior tested on both sides of a condition, not constants matching defaults.
- Actual native tool call, valid proposal, current root/profile/source identities. Full exact diff visible and source hashes unchanged while pending. Explicit digest-bound approval, **not Enter/Chat text or plan-mode approval**, authorizes the exact plan once.
- Commit only reviewed targets; preserve unrelated/pre-existing work; one-shot reviewed verification authority and original budgets. Reject/stale/duplicate/expired/wrong-session plan = no mutation. Success only from matching final source/build/required tests/current artifact facts; model prose cannot mint success.
- After successful edit, a second legitimate task uses a newly reviewed baseline revision. Refusing an outdated pin is correct safety but repeated manual hard-coded source edits are not considered a usable onboarding loop.

### B3 — build/test dan recovery yang dapat dipercaya

- A success task produces current build hashes/MVID + exact required TRX inventory and zero missing/skipped facts. Profile says what is tested; build exit0 or unrelated tests are insufficient.
- One controlled build/test failure exercises existing reverse recovery; record original failure and restored-baseline hashes, keep unrelated files unchanged. With an external edit conflict, refuse destructive recovery and show RecoveryConflict, not “restored”.
- Cancellation/deadline during execution drains only owned Job before verification/recovery conclusions. Another user app/independent process survives; no name/raw-PID cleanup. No Agent deadline refill or silent retry.
- Crash/interruption proof via owned test process on disposable data: reopen durable status/receipts, reconcile before/after/backup state, no grant resurrection or automatic duplicate write; fresh approval for a new continuation. This crash property is **required future Beta acceptance**, not claimed by historical I1 recovery.

### Shared acceptance gates

1. Targeted → impacted regression through pinned SDK `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Microsoft\dotnet\dotnet.exe` **8.0.424**, existing runsettings, approved isolated Job runner; record exact commands/filters/discovery/TRX, source and binary identities. Do not rerun all Gate H cases during feature audit.
2. Fresh actual WPF proof on final candidate: consent, review, send/stop, scope switch, readable failure/recovery/receipt, Light/Dark/High Contrast and keyboard accessibility relevant to those workflows. Scaled RenderTargetBitmap proof is not native OS-DPI/physical IME/screen-reader proof; classify outstanding items explicitly.
3. New current-source mandatory failures are triaged and resolved under approved contracts before certification; historical “pre-existing” does not waive mandatory acceptance. No assertion weakening or expanding P1 Option B exception.
4. A fresh release/source freeze and finite live budget need explicit owner approval. Historical consumed approvals, 331 PASS and the 47-test UI report do not certify current Beta automatically.

Cases: exact request hanya menambah dua observation flags; complete raw-byte preservation; native calls *reported* tanpa host acceptance/execution; call-like text tetap passive; dua historical captures tetap length failures dan instrumentation-insufficient; missing tokens/state; effective cap mismatch; HTTP500/invalid JSON; partial/overflow capture; duplicate JSON fields; recorded template props; exclusive output paths; live-origin disallowed. Tidak ada request HTTP/socket, model load, generation atau actual Agent run pada tests.

**Readiness:** **READY FOR ONE BOUNDED OBSERVATIONAL SCOPE APPROVAL**, dengan source-verified logging contract + offline-tested collector. **Belum runtime-verified logging completeness dan belum izin inference.** Jika future run tidak mengeluarkan fields yang dibutuhkan atau HTTP error memotong intermediate proof, catat `INSUFFICIENT`; jangan retry/top-up. Serialized arena/raw original sampled bytes yang tidak tersedia tidak boleh disimulasikan. Preflight cukup untuk observation question di atas, **tidak cukup untuk menjanjikan proof bahwa decoder salah menangani call valid**.

#### 11.10.2 Exact SOURCE pipeline graphs dan references

Graph berikut adalah **SOURCE**, dengan sebagian shipped-artifact support **STATIC** di §11.10.3, bukan observed live provider calls. Paths relatif masing-masing source repo/commit di atas; salinan source dan per-file URL/SHA ada di LA/source dan `*-fetch.json`. HTTP404 untuk obsolete guessed paths disimpan dan diganti dengan paths yang ditemukan dari exact-commit tree; tidak dijadikan successful references.

**Harness / inspected DeepSeek adapter path:**

```text
User inbox -> ReactLoopAgent.turn / step / prepareRequest
 -> ctx.llm.prepareCall -> DeepSeekAdapter.prepareCall / generate / request
 -> serialize(messages, tools, model route) -> POST .../messages -> parseSse / translate
 -> typed assistant tool-call blocks + terminal finish
 -> step: max-tokens stops before dispatch; otherwise executeToolCalls
 -> ToolRuntime prepare/dispatch/finalize -> appendToolResult/session log
 -> deriveMessages + next admitted step
```

- `packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:296,398–538,547–688`: request binding, immutable history, error/abort handling; **line530 max-tokens returns before tool dispatch**.
- `packages/llm/llm-deepseek/src/adapter.ts:43–73,103–147`: adapter-owned prepared route, cancellable request/idle watchdog and `/messages` SSE transport.
- `.../serialize.ts:130–166`: Messages `tool_use/tool_result` correlation, model/default max_tokens, tools `{name,description,input_schema}`. Ini bukan OpenAI chat function wrapper.
- `.../translate.ts:45–96,104–165`: `startBlock` membedakan native `tool_use` dari text; `stopReason` memisahkan `max_tokens`; incomplete/closed-stream errors. **Tidak menemukan transformasi ordinary call-like text menjadi native call pada path ini.**
- `packages/core/agent-loop/src/tool-calls.ts:60–110,148–158`: parsed arguments, bounded/exclusive scheduling, ordered durable results. JSON argument parse failure dapat dipertahankan sebagai raw diagnostic input; `core/tools/src/schema.ts:597–600` dan `index.ts:1369–1387` tetap menegakkan registry/schema/policy execution path. Itu bukan text-to-tool authority fallback.

**OpenCode default AI-SDK path, v1.19.3:**

```text
SessionPrompt.loop/process input -> Provider.resolveSDK/getLanguage
 -> LLM.run -> LLMRequestPrep.prepare/resolveTools -> streamText
 -> @ai-sdk/openai-compatible getArgs/prepareTools -> /chat/completions
 -> native tool_calls/deltas and finish -> LLMAISDK.toLLMEvents
 -> SDK registered execute callbacks + SessionProcessor state/results
 -> MessageV2.toModelMessagesEffect -> next prompt loop step
```

- `packages/opencode/src/provider/provider.ts:148–175,1788–1863`: explicit SDK loaders, route/baseURL/options and provider cache; capability/model config influences request. Actual user provider/flags tidak dibaca.
- `session/llm/request.ts:56–158,181–225`: system/prompt/options merge, permission-filtered tools, provider-specific strict:false for Responses-family tools, deterministic tool ordering.
- `session/llm.ts:85–120,227–260,280–343`: native LLM runtime adalah opt-in branch; **default source path** memakai `streamText`, abortSignal/maxRetries, tools/toolChoice/maxOutputTokens. Native opt-in implementation tidak ditelusuri sebagai default yang dianggap aktif pada user session.
- `session/llm/ai-sdk.ts:22–24,77–127,193–291`: typed fullStream events/finish normalization; `processor.ts:299–500,cleanup/process` menyimpan tool pending/running/result/error dan interrupted status. `prompt.ts:1255–1321,1343+` membentuk model history dan continuation.
- **Exact transport source:** release dependency `@ai-sdk/openai-compatible` **2.0.41**, Apache-2.0. Official npm source tarball SHA512 integrity diverifikasi terhadap registry metadata; hanya dibaca/diekstrak sebagai source, **tidak diinstall atau dieksekusi**. `src/chat/openai-compatible-prepare-tools.ts:59–88` dan `openai-compatible-chat-language-model.ts:184–280,303–320,522–648`: OpenAI function schema, max_tokens, string required/auto/none, native tool_call/delta decoding. `map-openai-compatible-finish-reason.ts:3–18` tetap melabeli `length` sebagai length; legacy `function_call` mapping bukan alasan memperlonggar pinned Nexus protocol.
- `experimental_repairToolCall` pada `llm.ts:296–311` bisa lowercase known tool name atau route ke invalid tool. Ini repair terhadap **already identified SDK tool-call failure**, bukan decoder untuk ordinary text. **Tidak diadopsi ke exact-name Nexus authority.** Streaming SDK dapat memunculkan complete tool events sebelum final finish; tidak menyalin perilaku dispatch timing itu ke Nexus.
- Desktop source `packages/desktop/src/main/sidecar.ts:51–65` imports `virtual:opencode-server` lalu `Server.listen`; `electron.vite.config.ts:6,65–77` binds generated Node server bundle. Ini carrier/backend link; bukan bukti semua provider chunks dijalankan atau cocok byte-for-byte dengan public source.

**Codex exact matched CLI release:**

```text
session::turn::run_turn -> ModelClientSession / build_responses_request
 -> create_tools_raw_json_for_responses_api -> ResponsesClient.stream_request
 -> /responses SSE (atau selected Responses WebSocket)
 -> sse::responses::process_event -> typed OutputItemDone
 -> handle_output_item_done -> ToolRouter::build_tool_call
 -> ToolRuntime.handle_tool_call -> ToolRegistry.dispatch_any_with_state
 -> correlated ResponseInputItem tool outputs/history -> needs_follow_up -> next sampling step
```

- `codex-rs/core/src/client.rs:956–1088,1756+,2368+`: request construction/route; `tools` are Responses tool specs, `tool_choice=auto`, stream=true; provider/model feature metadata controls reasoning/parallel/lite mode.
- `codex-rs/model-provider-info/src/lib.rs:100–131,656–686`: **Responses-only WireApi**, explicit error for `wire_api="chat"`; even bundled Ollama/LMStudio definitions use Responses in this release. Generic OpenAI-compatible *Chat* endpoint alone tidak cukup.
- `codex-rs/codex-api/src/endpoint/responses.rs:70–157`: `/responses`, SSE/idle timeout. `sse/responses.rs:354–477,~570`: typed output items, incomplete/error/stream-close propagation. Incomplete reasons selain explicit interrupted/content-filter handling tidak disamakan dengan successful complete response.
- `codex-rs/tools/src/responses_api.rs:32–80`, `tool_spec.rs`: typed function/custom/namespace schemas. Freeform **custom tool call** adalah native typed API item, **bukan assistant text yang dipromosikan menjadi tool**.
- `core/src/tools/router.rs:257–308`: `FunctionCall/CustomToolCall/ToolSearchCall` menjadi ToolCall; ordinary message/default `Ok(None)`. `stream_events_utils.rs:315–424` persists and queues call/typed error output; `tools/parallel.rs:78–126,~228`, `registry.rs:615–852` dispatch/cancel boundaries; `session/turn.rs:166+,2801–2910` typed item processing/needs-follow-up.

**Nexus current:** `SharedRuntimeAgent.ExecuteAsync` → existing lease/capability admission → `AgentOrchestrator.ExecuteCoreAsync:50–86` host-selected descriptors → `NativeToolModelAdapter.GenerateAsync:24–80` → non-stream OpenAI chat endpoint → `NativeToolProtocol.Parse:20–61` → `SourceProposalScope.Accept` → existing executor/exact-plan approval/SafeWrite/verification/recovery → `AssistantMessage/ToolMessage:69–80` correlated history → finite follow-up/final facts verification. `SharedRuntimeAgent:89–94,191–202` linked cancellation/deadline and terminal classification; `NativeToolCapability:20–37` exact pinned identity gate. No second runtime/parser introduced.

#### 11.10.4 Harness vs OpenCode vs Codex vs Nexus

| Concern | Harness inspected route | OpenCode inspected route | Codex matched CLI | Nexus / engineering implication |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Request/schema | Messages `{name,input_schema}`, provider-owned reasoning/default output limits | OpenAI-compatible `{type:function,function:{name,description,parameters}}`; SDK `max_tokens` and string tool_choice | Responses function/custom/namespace tool specs, input items, auto choice | **Nexus chat function-schema placement matches OpenCode SDK source.** Cloud format differences bukan proof bug. |
| Native vs call-like text | typed tool_use block only | native tool_calls/deltas only on audited SDK path; name repair optional | typed FunctionCall/CustomToolCall items; ordinary Message not ToolCall | Keep strict native envelope; never use pseudo-call text as authority. |
| Finish/incomplete | max-tokens guard before dispatch; malformed/closed stream fails | finish length retained; SDK events may precede terminal finish | incomplete/error typed; explicit interrupted continuation is distinct | Nexus terminal check before any dispatch is intentional fail-closed. Do not copy partial/streaming dispatch for a mutation path. |
| Capability selection | bound prepared adapter/route capabilities, scoped registry | model/provider catalog/config/API route, native opt-in or AI-SDK path | provider Responses capabilities + model ToolMode/features | Identity/capability metadata is not empirically proven task reliability in any client. Extend explicit C# metadata only in future approved scope. |
| Results/continuation | durable call/result log, deriveMessages | persisted ToolParts → model history, next loop | typed response input outputs + needs_follow_up | Reuse correlation and honest outcome separation; keep finite Nexus budgets and final host fact verification. |
| Timeout/cancel | adapter idle watchdog/AbortSignal + tool scheduler drains | abortSignal/Effect interruption, provider timeout adapter and cleanup | SSE idle timeout/CancellationToken/.or_cancel_tool | Preserve Nexus hard120, linked runtime lease, mandatory mutation recovery drain and owned Job; no retry/budget top-up from reference defaults. |
| Decoder ownership | remote provider native Messages; agent validates/maps API blocks | local/cloud provider runtime emits native envelope; SDK assembles/maps it | provider Responses wire events; client maps typed items | Gemma token/template decoder remains b11057 responsibility. A more capable client cannot establish a tool call absent from native output without a different, separately reviewed contract. |
| llama.cpp compatibility | Audited DeepSeek Messages adapter is **not** direct chat drop-in; other plugins not proven here | OpenAI-compatible chat route is closest structural match; **not tested with this Gemma/b11057** | Responses endpoint required; generic chat-only compatibility insufficient | No installed-agent Gemma success proof. Do not point Codex-style Responses at Nexus chat endpoint or transplant Node/Electron runtime. |

#### 11.10.5 Reuse decisions A–D

**A — directly useful within existing Nexus services:** native/text distinction, correlated call/result identity, explicit finish/incomplete outcome and source-vs-runtime evidence separation. D1 and current collector already enforce relevant parts; do not rewrite working components just to mirror OSS.

**B — ADAPT to C#:** protocol/route/capability manifest instead of assuming every OpenAI-like endpoint identical; provider-attempt/wire/proposal/host-verification diagnostics as separate facts; deterministic bounded schema presentation; future durable trajectory. These are small changes at existing adapter/orchestrator seams after exact scope approval, not a new framework/parser or unrestricted executor.

**C — provider-specific, not local Gemma proof:** Messages/Responses wire shape, thinking/cache/replay semantics, tool-choice defaults, cloud strict:false, custom/freeform typed tools, legacy finish mappings, name repair, streaming parallel dispatch and permissive continuation. **Do not adopt** aliases/fallbacks that weaken Nexus exact-name/schema/authority or use larger reference caps as evidence to raise native256.

**D — KEEP Nexus stronger boundaries:** opaque expiring read grants/session/root fences; host-created profile/proposal range/hash constraints; exact independent RunId/PlanId/digest one-shot approval; no mutation until accepted complete native request and actual plan approval; SafeWrite/graph/artifact verification/required tests/recovery; shared production runtime owner, fixed SDK and Job/deadline budgets. Generic provider approval or tool schema is not an equivalent safety authority.

#### 11.10.6 Answers to I2-B decision questions and one next action

1. **Concrete request differences exist:** transport family, stream mode, required-vs-auto choice, capability/default limits, optional schema/name normalization. Closest OpenAI-compatible source uses **same function-wrapper/parameters and max_tokens/string required layout** as Nexus. Absent optional fields are not automatically defects; named-object tool choice must not be copied over pinned b11057's reviewed string-selection contract.
2. **No new causal Nexus request-construction error established offline** for the two captured length/no-call failures. D1's known-good native envelope acceptance and historical rejection remain evidence; not a theorem every future response is bug-free. S1 and four open debts remain separate from native generation cause.
3. **Template/runtime/provider distinction remains material and unresolved.** All inspected clients principally consume provider-native typed events. Source contracts do not reproduce the missing b11057 intermediate state or demonstrate this Gemma generates correct calls.
4. **One prepared generation/termination diagnostic remains necessary and still not authorized.** Do not add another decoder experiment or clone a reference client. Existing GP collector/prepared request §11.7 remain unchanged and 14/14 result is reused, not rerun for this study.
5. **Alternative adapter route worth separate evaluation:** OpenAI-compatible chat is closest to current Nexus; a model/provider with declared native support could be tested through existing seams with pinned identity, same negative/proposal/semantic acceptance and fresh finite scope. Codex's Responses route is viable only with a proven Responses-capable endpoint, not a drop-in for chat compatibility. No alternative model/provider selected/tested or production runtime changed here; §11.7 branch-C plan remains the fallback.

**Satu next action:** owner review/approval atas **existing §11.7 max1 native observation**: b11057/exact GGUF, cap256/hard120, zero Agent/executor/writer/approval/source mutation, logging-only flags, isolated Job, no retry/top-up. This study itself consumed **0 model generations /0 actual Agent runs**. Diagnostic outcome—not more reference archaeology—must guide the next bounded engineering decision.

Historical immutable reports/seals/archives, source and staged changes are not edited. Snapshot artifacts remain externally by hash; raw REA/source/version receipts have unique paths. Four failures §11.6 remain unresolved and not excluded from future certification. **I2-B HOLD; Gate J HOLD.**

#### 11.11.3 Kemampuan yang Lebih Baik dari Nexus

| Area Kemampuan | Implementasi Unsloth Desktop (OSS) | Gap pada Nexus C# Saat Ini | Nilai Tambah bagi Nexus |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Response-Side Tool Healing** | `passthrough_healing.py`: Mengubah panggilan fungsi yang dipancarkan model sebagai teks markup (`<tool_call>`, Gemma `<|tool_call>`, `[TOOL_CALLS]`, `<function=>`) menjadi structured `tool_calls` pada sisi respons tanpa menyentuh request prompt atau mengacaukan KV-cache. | Nexus menolak secara kaku semua respons yang tidak memiliki `tool_calls` native di envelope JSON (`MissingNativeToolCall`). Jika model mengalirkan teks, turn langsung gagal. | Memulihkan tool calling pada model kecil/lokal (Gemma, Llama, Mistral) yang sering mengalami kegagalan emit native envelope. |
| **Gemma 4 Native Dialect Support** | `tool_healing.py` & `tool_call_parser.py`: Parser dan normalizer lengkap untuk dialek unik Gemma 4 (`<|tool_call>call:NAME{...}<tool_call|>` dan bare `call:NAME{...}`), token kutip string `<|"|>`, unquoted object keys, dan bare array elements. | Nexus tidak memiliki normalizer dialek Gemma. Karakter token khusus `<|"|>` dan format JSON non-standar Gemma langsung menggagalkan parser `NativeToolProtocol`. | Menghilangkan friksi parser sintaks saat Nexus menjalankan model Gemma 4 lokal. |
| **Broken JSON Guard (`_looks_like_broken_json`)** | `tool_loop_controller.py:51–84`: Membedakan secara akurat antara bare string yang valid di-heal dan JSON yang terpotong di tengah jalan (cut-off mid-stream). Menolak mempromosikan JSON yang terputus menjadi eksekusi program. | Nexus mendeteksi `length` hanya di level envelope wire (`finish_reason == "length"`), tetapi tidak memiliki parser diagnostik internal untuk mendeteksi derajat fragmentasi payload. | Memastikan integritas SafeWrite: potongan kode yang tidak lengkap tidak akan pernah dibungkus menjadi argumen proposal palsu. |
| **Atomic Tool Context Compaction** | `context_window.py:110–128`: `group_turns` memperlakukan pasangan pesan asisten (`tool_calls`) dan hasil eksekusi (`tool`) sebagai **satu unit atomik** saat rolling context window melakukan eviksi. | Nexus memotong riwayat percakapan secara leksikal/flat tanpa perlindungan atomik siklus tool-result. | Mencegah rusaknya percakapan OpenAI-compatible / desinkronisasi KV-cache llama.cpp akibat pemotongan parsial pasangan panggilan tool. |
| **Admission Queue & Slot Balancing** | `llama_admission.py:47–67`: Antrean FIFO dengan pembatasan token dan kuota KV-cache per-slot ketika melayani beberapa inferensi bersamaan. | Nexus menggunakan lock biner sederhana pada runtime lease. | Memungkinkan eksekusi background agent yang lebih resilien tanpa menyebabkan OOM atau slot contention pada llama.cpp. |
| **Vulkan GPU & Parameter Profiles** | `llama_server_args.py` & `UNSLOTH_PREBUILT_INFO.json`: Profil otomatisasi argumen llama.cpp untuk akselerasi Intel/AMD GPU melalui Vulkan runtime (`ggml-vulkan.dll`). | Nexus masih mengandalkan konfigurasi llama.cpp b11057 yang terikat pada build CPU/eksplisit. | Memberikan kecepatan inferensi lokal yang jauh lebih tinggi pada laptop/PC pengguna dengan memanfaatkan iGPU Vulkan terpasang. |

#### 11.11.4 Exact Source yang Layak Digunakan & Keputusan Arsitektur

Sesuai urutan prioritas **USE → PORT → ADAPT → REPLACE → KEEP → REMOVE**:

1. **USE (Gunakan Langsung):**
   - **Algoritma Normalisasi String Murni:** Gunakan langsung spesifikasi logika normalisasi string dan regex parsing dialek tool-call dari source Unsloth yang terverifikasi (bebas side-effect dan murni deterministik).
   - *Catatan Otoritas Runtime:* Binary eksternal bundled llama.cpp b11160 Vulkan (`C:\Users\Syand\.unsloth\llama.cpp\build\bin\Release`) **TIDAK** diperlakukan sebagai runtime drop-in terbukti. Otoritas runtime lokal Nexus tetap dipatok (pinned) pada engine b11057 terverifikasi; setiap evaluasi runtime baru berada pada status **HOLD** dan memerlukan sertifikasi gate terpisah.

2. **PORT (Porting ke C#):**
   - **`GemmaToolCallDialect` (dari `core/tool_healing.py`):** Porting fungsi normalisasi string Gemma (`_normalise_gemma_quoted_strings`, token `<|"|>`, unquoted keys) ke C# untuk ditempatkan di `Nexus.App.Services.Agent.GemmaDialectNormalizer`.
   - **`BrokenJsonDetector` (dari `core/inference/tool_loop_controller.py`):** Porting logika `_looks_like_broken_json` ke C# untuk mendeteksi JSON terputus di level token, mencegah payload terpotong dipromosikan ke eksekusi.
   - **`TextToolCallClassifier` (dari `core/inference/passthrough_healing.py`):** Porting `classify` diagnostik ke C# untuk mengklasifikasikan respons lokal model secara objektif (`NativeEnvelopeObserved`, `IncompleteGeneration`, `TextCallCandidate`, `NoNativeCall`).

3. **ADAPT (Sesuaikan dengan Arsitektur Nexus):**
   - **`AtomicToolTurnCompaction` (dari `core/inference/context_window.py`):** Sesuaikan `group_turns` ke dalam sistem penyimpanan riwayat chat Nexus (`ConversationViewModel` / `ConversationStore`) agar riwayat asisten tool-call dan tool-result tidak terpisah saat terjadi pemangkasan konteks.
   - **`LlamaServerArgBuilder` (dari `core/inference/llama_server_args.py`):** Sesuaikan argumen denylist dan profil flag Vulkan ke dalam service spawning llama.cpp di Nexus (`LlamaServerManager`).

4. **REPLACE (Ganti Komponen Nexus yang Usang):**
   - Ganti penolakan biner buta pada adapter model lokal dengan **Diagnostic Layer Terstruktur** yang mampu melaporkan penyebab spesifik jika model mengeluarkan teks alih-alih native call.
   - Ganti deteksi capability hardcoded satu model dengan Model Profile berbasis capability flags (dukungan thinking, dialek tool-call, batas konteks).

5. **KEEP (Pertahankan Komponen Nexus yang Terbukti Lebih Aman & Matang):**
   - **Exact-Plan Approval (`AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider`):** Otoritas eksekusi tetap mutlak di tangan pengguna melalui dialog approval satu kali dengan digest PlanId yang tidak dapat dipalsukan.
   - **Host-Issued Permissions & Opaque Grants:** Batas baca proyek yang kedaluwarsa otomatis (10 menit) dan terisolasi per-root folder.
   - **SafeWrite & Hash-Guarded Reverse Recovery:** Penulisan berkas aman dengan backup byte-for-byte dan pemulihan otomatis jika kompilasi/tes gagal.
   - **Fixed Verification Engine:** Verifikasi 3 fakta mutlak (source hash, build bersih, unit test lolos) di lingkungan terisolasi LocalLow.
   - **Windows Job Object Process Lifecycle:** Kepemilikan proses compiler dan worker berbasis Job Object Windows (secara teknis jauh lebih aman dan bersih daripada pemanggilan `Popen.terminate()` milik Python di lingkungan Windows).
   - **Native C# / WPF Independence:** Nexus tetap beroperasi penuh sebagai aplikasi native C#/.NET 8 murni tanpa mewajibkan dependency runtime Python bagi pengguna akhir.

6. **REMOVE (Hilangkan dari Scope & Klaim):**
   - Eliminasi klaim kesamaan tampilan kosmetik (cosmetic parity) yang tidak berdampak pada kapabilitas.
   - Hapus ekspektasi bahwa prompt engineering semata dapat memperbaiki kelemahan decoding sintaks model lokal tanpa layer normalisasi.

#### 11.11.5 Evaluasi Kritis I2-B: Text-Healing vs OutputLimit

Penyelidikan mendalam terhadap `passthrough_healing.py` dan `tool_loop_controller.py` memberikan kejelasan tuntas mengenai insiden I2-B historis:

1. **Mengapa I2-B Mengeluarkan Teks Markup Gemma:**
   Gemma-4-E4B pada llama.cpp b11057 secara alami memancarkan token `<|tool_call>call:nexus_apply_source_plan{...}<tool_call|>` dengan string `<|"|>`. Tanpa template Jinja server-side yang secara eksplisit memetakan dialek tersebut ke envelope OpenAI JSON, llama.cpp mengeluarkan teks markup mentah di dalam `message.content` dan membiarkan `tool_calls` bernilai `null`.

2. **Batas Keamanan Text-Healing Terhadap `finish=length`:**
   Pada kedua run I2-B yang gagal (`3cc9bb20...` dan `0020ccb4...`), model mencapai batas `max_tokens = 256` sehingga `finish_reason` adalah `length`. 
   Source code Unsloth sendiri (`tool_loop_controller.py:702–720`) secara eksplisit menegaskan bahwa:
   > *"Healing exists for a model that sends its ONE argument as a bare string instead of an object. Text that opens like JSON and fails to parse is not that -- it is a broken object, usually one cut off mid-stream, and wrapping it whole becomes the argument's value... on a call whose real problem was that its JSON never finished arriving."*
   
   Oleh karena itu, **Nexus mengambil keputusan keamanan yang benar**: respons yang berakhir `length` adalah generasi tidak lengkap. Text-healing **tidak boleh dan tidak akan pernah** mengubah potongan teks tidak lengkap menjadi otoritas eksekusi SafeWrite.
   
3. **Klarifikasi Batas Kebijakan I2-B: Peningkatan Cap & Runtime Switch Bukan Solusi Terbukti:**
   - **Peningkatan `max_tokens` Bukan Solusi Terbukti:** Mengusulkan penaikan cap token (misalnya ke 1024 atau 2048) bukanlah solusi terbukti. Token budget 256 tokens pada I2-B adalah batas pagar (budget fence) kebijakan yang ketat untuk mencegah divergensi dan runaway, serta penolakan `OutputLimit` adalah perlindungan keamanan mandatory di bawah Gate H Option B / Gate I. Menaikkan cap token melanggar budget fence dan tidak menyelesaikan akar masalah desinkronisasi template/gramatika model.
   - **Perpindahan ke b11160 Vulkan Bukan Solusi Terbukti:** Mengganti binary runtime ke b11160 Vulkan adalah hipotesis eksternal yang belum teruji, bukan perbaikan terbukti. Runtime switch berada pada status **HOLD** tanpa bukti sertifikasi deterministik pada harness Nexus.
   - **Pertahankan Strict Native Envelope & Penolakan OutputLimit:** Nexus tetap memberlakukan penolakan kaku (fail-closed) terhadap `OutputLimit` (`finish=length`) dan ketiadaan native envelope. Otoritas eksekusi SafeWrite, exact approval, verifikasi, dan recovery tidak boleh dikompromikan.
   - **Diagnostic-Only Scope:** Deteksi dialek teks Gemma yang dipelajari dari Unsloth hanya diizinkan beroperasi sebagai *diagnostic-only classifier* (pelaporan observabilitas). Text-healed call **dilarang dipromosikan otomatis** menjadi executable native tool call yang memegang hak eksekusi file system.

#### 11.11.6 Prioritas Implementation Slice: `NexusGemmaDialectDiagnostic`

Sebagai langkah konkret pertama (Phase C) yang aman, terkendali, dan bernilai guna tinggi:
1. **Target Berkas:**
   - Service: `Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/Agent/GemmaDialectNormalizer.cs`
   - Model: `Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/Agent/ModelResponseClassifier.cs`
   - Tests: `Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/GemmaDialectCompatibilityTests.cs`
2. **Cakupan Fungsional:**
   - Implementasikan fungsi murni (pure C#) tanpa side-effect untuk normalisasi sintaks token Gemma (`<|"|>`, `call:name{...}`).
   - Implementasikan deteksi `BrokenJsonDetector` untuk mendeteksi string JSON yang terputus mid-token.
   - Integrasikan klasifikasi diagnostik ke dalam penanganan respons adapter model lokal (Diagnostic-Only: mengklasifikasikan respons untuk telemetri/observabilitas; TIDAK memberikan execution authority atau mempromosikan teks ke native dispatcher).
3. **Kriteria Sukses Verifikasi:**
   - Tes offline memvalidasi bahwa payload Gemma yang lengkap berhasil dinormalisasi menjadi JSON standar.
   - Tes offline memvalidasi bahwa payload yang terpotong (`finish=length` atau broken JSON) tetap **ditolak secara fail-closed** dan diklasifikasikan sebagai `IncompleteGeneration`.
   - Zero side-effects pada disk atau file proyek nyata selama pengujian.

#### 11.11.7 Status Eksekusi & Bukti Verifikasi Phase C (`ModelResponseClassifier`)

Sesuai persetujuan owner pada sesi 17/10/2026:
1. **Komponen yang Diimplementasikan:**
   - `Nexus.App.Services.Agent.ModelResponseClassifier`: Utilitas diagnostik mandiri (*standalone diagnostic utility*) pure C#/.NET 8 (tanpa side-effect, tanpa dependensi Python). Menggabungkan klasifikasi status respons (`IncompleteGeneration`, `NativeEnvelopeObserved`, `TextMarkupGemmaCandidate`, `PlainProse`), normalisasi token kutip Gemma (`<|"|>`), quoting unquoted keys, dan deteksi broken/truncated JSON (`LooksLikeBrokenJson`).
   - *Catatan Integrasi Runtime:* Utilitas ini **BELUM dipanggil oleh production adapter (`NativeToolModelAdapter`)** dan beroperasi murni secara offline/terisolasi. Tidak ada integrasi produksi yang ditambahkan secara prematur tanpa persetujuan scope terpisah. Observabilitas runtime live belum berubah.
   - `Nexus.Integration.Tests.GemmaDialectCompatibilityTests`: Rangkaian 6 skenario TDD offline terisolasi.
2. **Hasil Verifikasi Pinned .NET SDK & Windows Job Object Sandbox:**
   - **Targeted Diagnostic Tests:** **9/9 PASS**, 0 Failed, 0 Skipped (TRX terverifikasi: `gemma-dialect-tests.trx`, 17.797 bytes).
   - **Impacted Regression Suite:** **36/36 PASS** mencakup `I2BNativeCompatibilityReplayTests`, `ToolProtocolTests`, dan `GemmaDialectCompatibilityTests` (TRX terverifikasi: `impacted-regression-tests.trx`, 60.869 bytes).
   - *Status Hutang Regresi:* Hasil 36/36 PASS ini **TIDAK menutup 4 regression failures historis (§11.6)**; keempat kasus tersebut tetap dipertahankan sebagai *unresolved debt*.
   - **Isolasi Output:** Seluruh kompilasi diarahkan ke direktori terisolasi `bin-gemma-diagnostic`. Biner default `bin/Debug/net8.0-windows/` 100% tidak tersentuh.
3. **Kepastian Keamanan Fail-Closed & Otoritas Eksekusi:**
   - Kedua rekaman historis I2-B (`native-1-response.json` dan `native-2-response.json`) tetap terbukti ditolak secara kaku sebagai `OutputLimit` dan diklasifikasikan sebagai `IncompleteGeneration`.
   - **Diferensiasi Semantik:** Pengenalan sintaks (*syntax recognition*) dan validitas struktur (*structural validity*) dari argumen Gemma yang dinormalisasi **BUKAN bukti kebenaran semantik atau fungsional tugas (*task semantic & behavioral correctness*)**. Sebagaimana ditegaskan oleh `I2BNativeCompatibilityReplayTests.StructurallyValidProposal_IsNotTaskSemanticVerification`, lolosnya struktur proposal bukan verifikasi semantik.
   - Deteksi markup Gemma (`<|tool_call>...<tool_call|>`) beroperasi murni sebagai kandidat diagnostik observabilitas (`TextMarkupGemmaCandidate`). Parser `NativeToolProtocol` tetap mengembalikan `ToolCall == null` dan menolak turn sebagai `MissingNativeToolCall` jika tool diwajibkan.
   - Klasifikasi diagnostik **tidak mengubah keputusan `NativeToolProtocol`, `OutputLimit`, approval, dispatcher, SafeWrite maupun reverse recovery**.
   - Terbukti secara formal: **Zero dispatch, zero approval request, dan zero workspace disk mutation**.
4. **Status Kebijakan & Keputusan Lanjutan Pasca-§11.7:**
   - **Eksekusi Live Diagnostic §11.7 SELESAI:** Satu native generation telah dikonsumsi pada 17 Oktober 2026 (`completion_tokens=256`, `finish_reason="length"`, `tool_calls=null`, inversi semantik `IsCpu`). Tidak ada re-run atau retry yang diizinkan.
   - **I2-B Native Acceptance: HOLD.** Belum ada bukti lolosnya native tool-calling envelope dan semantik kode yang benar pada model Gemma-4-E4B di llama.cpp b11057.
   - **Gate J: HOLD.** Menunggu kepastian jalur native tool calling.
   - **Hutang Regresi Historis (§11.6): TETAP OPEN.** Empat kegagalan regresi dipertahankan sebagai technical debt yang belum tertutup.
   - **Decoder Archaeology C++ (D1–D3): DITUTUP RESMI.**
   - **Langkah Resmi Berikutnya:** Alternative Model/Provider Compatibility Evaluation (§11.13) dan Perumusan Arsitektur Agent Serbaguna (§11.12).

### 11.12 Arsitektur Agent Serbaguna & Matriks Komprehensif OSS Reuse (Target Personal Beta)

Sesuai arahan owner pada 17 Oktober 2026, Nexus diposisikan sebagai **AI Agent pribadi serbaguna (*general-purpose personal AI agent*) berbasis native C#/.NET 8/WPF**, bukan sekadar asisten penyunting kode atau aplikasi obrolan teks.

#### 11.12.1 Prinsip Implementasi & Doktrin Arsitektur

Nexus menerapkan hierarki pemanfaatan open-source secara ketat:
$$\text{KEEP} \longrightarrow \text{USE} \longrightarrow \text{PORT} \longrightarrow \text{ADAPT} \longrightarrow \text{REPLACE} \longrightarrow \text{REMOVE} \longrightarrow \text{DEFER}$$

- **Tunggal & Kohesif:** Tidak boleh membangun runtime manager, agent orchestrator, tool executor, workspace writer, atau security policy engine kedua. Komponen lama yang digantikan harus dimigrasikan secara utuh tanpa meninggalkan dead code atau merusak consumer/test yang ada.
- **Model Mengusulkan, Nexus Memutuskan:** Model bahasa bebas mengusulkan pemanggilan tool, namun **Nexus Host memegang seluruh otoritas izin, validasi, dan eksekusi**.
- **Isolasi Izin (*Permission Fencing*):** Izin dipisahkan secara granular:
  - `Read`: Membaca berkas proyek, metadata, dokumen yang disetujui (bounded catalog/grant).
  - `Write`: Modifikasi berkas lokal (wajib melalui immutable diff review, digest plan approval, dan SafeWrite).
  - `Execute`: Eksekusi compiler, unit test, proses luar (terisolasi dalam Windows Job Object, tanpa unrestricted shell).
  - `Network`: Akses eksternal/web/MCP (hanya melalui provider dan izin spesifik yang diberikan eksplisit).
  Keberadaan atau pendaftaran tool dalam katalog **tidak otomatis memberikan izin eksekusi**.

#### 11.12.2 Fondasi Keamanan & Integritas Nexus (Wajib Dipertahankan / KEEP)

Komponen inti berikut telah terbukti secara empiris memberikan jaminan keamanan lebih kuat daripada implementasi OSS berbasis Electron/Node/Python dan **WAJIB DIPERTAHANKAN**:

1. **Host-Issued Permissions & Project Consent:** Akses baca proyek berbasis grant dengan TTL pendek (10 menit) dan batas root direktori terisolasi (`ProjectReadContracts.cs`).
2. **Strict Native Tool Protocol:** Parser fail-closed (`NativeToolProtocol.cs`) yang menolak duplicate properties, format non-standar, argumen melampaui batas byte, dan menolak konversi otomatis teks bebas menjadi eksekusi program.
3. **Exact Immutable Diff Approval:** Hak eksekusi perubahan kode sepenuhnya di tangan pengguna melalui dialog persetujuan satu kali dengan verifikasi PlanId dan SHA-256 digest (`AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider.cs`).
4. **SafeWrite Engine:** Penulisan berkas aman dengan pembuatan cadangan byte-for-byte dan pemulihan otomatis jika kompilasi atau tes gagal (`AgentSafeWrite.cs`).
5. **Deterministic 3-Fact Verification Engine:** Verifikasi absolut terhadap 3 fakta empiris (source hash terverifikasi, build kompilasi bersih, unit test lolos) sebelum perubahan dianggap selesai (`SourceVerification.cs`).
6. **Hash-Guarded Reverse Recovery:** Mekanisme pemulihan otomatis yang mengembalikan kondisi berkas ke status awal jika terjadi kesalahan atau konflik (`EditingWorkflowService.SourcePlan.cs`).
7. **Windows Job Object Process Lifecycle:** Kepemilikan proses kompilator SDK dan engine lokal menggunakan Windows Job Object API dengan flag `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`, mencegah proses liar/orphan pada sistem operasi pengguna.
8. **Single-Writer Preservation:** Penjaminan konkurensi bahwa hanya ada satu thread/penulis aktif pada repositori dalam satu waktu.
9. **Bounded Resource Budgets & Cancellation:** Pembatasan ketat token maksimum, batas byte request/response (65.536 bytes), batas argumen (8.192 bytes), timeout operasi (hard 120s), dan propagasi `CancellationToken` secara menyeluruh.

#### 11.12.3 Kategori Tool Target Agent Serbaguna

Arsitektur tool Nexus diperluas untuk mencakup skenario kerja harian personal tanpa mengorbankan isolasi keamanan:

| Kategori Tool | Cakupan Kapabilitas | Batas Izin & Kebijakan Nexus | Sumber Inspirasi OSS |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1. Repository Exploration** | List direktori, file tree, search/grep pola teks leksikal, membaca konten berkas yang disetujui. | `Read` saja. Path-traversal protection, secret file exclusions, output truncation limit. | DeepSeek Harness `tool-fs-search`, OpenCode file readers. |
| **2. Code Editing & Mutation** | Pembuatan rencana edit (proposal), perbandingan diff terstruktur, aplikasi perubahan berkas. | `Write` (wajib diff approval pengguna + PlanId digest). Transaksional per-turn. | Nexus existing SafeWrite + Codex structured file edit semantics. |
| **3. Build & Test Execution** | Menjalankan build dotnet, mengeksekusi test runner tertarget, menangkap output diagnostik compiler. | `Execute` (terisolasi dalam Windows Job Object). Argumen pinned, tanpa shell bebas. | Nexus existing `SourceVerification` + Harness build runner concepts. |
| **4. Task Planning & State** | Bounded multi-step checklist, perumusan langkah kerja, pelaporan kemajuan bertahap. | Internal state agent. Tidak memiliki efek samping pada filesystem. | DeepSeek Harness `plan-mode` (reviewable guidance, bukan unrestricted executor). |
| **5. Document & Data Processing** | Ekstraksi teks dari PDF, dokumen pengguna yang dipilih, inspeksi OCR terarah. | `Read` (eksplisit pada berkas input pengguna yang dibuka di tab dokumen). | Nexus existing `PdfUtilities` / Image subsystem + Unsloth data recipes. |
| **6. Web & Knowledge Research** | Pencarian referensi daring, ekstraksi dokumentasi publik, pengambilan konteks API. | `Network` (memerlukan toggle consent aktif, domain allowlist, bounded download budget). | OpenCode web search adapter + Harness research workflows. |
| **7. External Tools / MCP** | Integrasi tools eksternal terstandarisasi (MCP servers) yang direview dan dipercaya. | `Execute` + `Network` terisolasi per server. Skema divalidasi ketat oleh host, zero auto-trust. | OpenCode MCP client + DeepSeek Harness `mcp-client`. |
| **8. Session & Trajectory** | Riwayat interaksi percakapan tahan-crash, penyimpanan checkpoint kerja, resume tugas. | Append-only journal lokal pada LocalLow. Tanpa upload cloud. | DeepSeek Harness `session-persistence-jsonl` + Codex trajectory concepts. |

#### 11.12.4 Matriks Komprehensif OSS Reuse (4 Referensi: Unsloth, Harness, OpenCode, Codex)

| Komponen / Fungsionalitas | Implementasi Existing Nexus | Pola Terbaik OSS Referensi | Keputusan | Tindakan Arsitektur & Pagar Batas Keamanan |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Execution Approval Authority** | `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider` (PlanId digest, one-shot immutable prompt) | Harness: plan-mode & approval flags; OpenCode: tool confirmation; Codex: tool approval prompts | **KEEP NEXUS** | Pertahankan otoritas approval di host WPF. Jangan melemahkan verifikasi digest menjadi sekadar konfirmasi teks "yes/y". |
| **Repository File Search** | Static catalog leksikal sempit (`ProjectReadContextWorkflow`) | Harness: `search-core.ts` (glob/grep, ripgrep packaging); OpenCode: search provider | **ADAPT OSS** | Porting search/glob/grep terikat izin ke C#; batasi output size, cegah symlink traversal, kecualikan git/secrets. |
| **File Editing Engine** | `EditingWorkflowService.SourcePlan.cs` (2 slot fixed target) | Harness: `tool-str-replace-editor`; Codex: multi-file structured edits | **KEEP NEXUS** (Core) + **ADAPT OSS** (Scope) | Pertahankan SafeWrite dan reverse recovery. Perluas batas target dari 2 slot sempit menjadi arbitrary approved files pada project yang di-onboard. |
| **Process Lifecycle Management** | Pinned Windows Job Object (`KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`) | Harness: Node child_process; Unsloth: Python Popen.terminate | **KEEP NEXUS** | Job Object berbasis Win32 jauh lebih unggul dan bersih dalam membersihkan compiler dan server child process di Windows. |
| **Local Inference Runtime** | Pinned llama.cpp b11057 (CPU/explicit) | Unsloth: b11160 Vulkan bundled runtime + arg builder + GPU parameter profiles | **ADAPT OSS** | Adopsi parameter tuning dan profil hardware dari Unsloth (`llama_server_args.py`). Runtime switch b11160 tetap HOLD hingga lulus gate. |
| **Response Classification & Healing** | Blind fail-closed rejection (`MissingNativeToolCall`) | Unsloth: `passthrough_healing.py`, `tool_call_parser.py`, broken-JSON guards | **PORT OSS** (Diagnostic) | `ModelResponseClassifier` telah di-porting (36/36 PASS). Tetap diagnostic-only; dilarang mempromosikan teks healing ke executable authority. |
| **Context Compaction** | Pemotongan riwayat linier/flat leksikal | Unsloth: `context_window.py:group_turns` (atomic tool_call & tool_result pairing) | **PORT OSS** | Porting pengelompokan pasangan turn atomik ke C# agar rolling context tidak memutuskan korelasi OpenAI `tool_call_id`. |
| **Tool Protocol & Schemas** | OpenAI-compatible function schema placement di `NativeToolModelAdapter` | OpenCode: `@ai-sdk/openai-compatible` tool schemas; Codex: typed Response API items | **KEEP NEXUS** + **USE OSS** | Format schema Nexus sudah cocok dengan OpenCode. Pertahankan envelope native OpenAI standar. |
| **Planning & Checklist** | Finite orchestrator state | Harness: `plan-mode` (reviewable durable plan items); Codex: task planning items | **ADAPT OSS** | Implementasikan task checklist sederhana di Agent Orchestrator untuk tracking pekerjaan bertahap; plan approval bukan execution grant. |
| **Session Trajectory Journaling** | Transient memory + chat log flat (`ConversationStore`) | Harness: `session-persistence-jsonl` (append log, torn-tail recovery) | **ADAPT OSS** | Simpan riwayat eksekusi Agent dalam format append-only JSONL lokal yang memisahkan intent, tool call, hasil verifikasi, dan hash. |
| **Dynamic Plugin / NPM Marketplace** | Tidak ada | Harness: Cordis plugin architecture; OpenCode: dynamic tools | **REMOVE** | Dihapus dari roadmap Personal Beta. Nexus tidak memerlukan runtime packaging Node/NPM atau dynamic plugin execution. |
| **Unrestricted Terminal Shell** | Tidak ada | Harness: shell provider (pwsh/bash) | **REMOVE** | Dihapus secara tegas. Eksekusi kode hanya melalui compiler/test runner terverifikasi, bukan arbitrary interactive shell. |

#### 11.12.5 Penyesuaian Sasaran Gate J–M Pasca-I2-B (Progression Path)

1. **Gate J — Verified Read-Only Repository Exploration & Context Discovery:**
   - Menyediakan toolset eksplorasi aman: `nexus_list_directory`, `nexus_search_text`, `nexus_read_file_range`.
   - Terikat pada otorisasi JIT consent pengguna dan batasan direktori root repositori yang dipilih.
   - Pagar batas: Output limit 16 KB, timeout 15 detik, secret filtering (.env, .git, tokens), zero file mutations.
2. **Gate K — Generalized Reviewed-Project Coding & Safe Execution:**
   - Generalisasi penyuntingan kode Nexus melampaui 2 target compiled-in ke proyek repositori apa pun yang telah di-onboard dan disetujui.
   - Eksekusi alur: Proposal multi-file → Diff Review UI pengguna → One-shot Plan Approval → SafeWrite → SDK Compile & Test → Hash Verification.
   - Pagar batas: Rollback otomatis jika kompilasi gagal, isolated compilation cache.
3. **Gate L — Durable Trajectory, Receipts, Planning, and Safe Recovery:**
   - Journaling trajectory interaksi Agent dalam format append-only JSONL lokal.
   - Persistensi checkpoint: pemulihan konteks kerja pasca-restart aplikasi tanpa kehilangan riwayat persetujuan atau integritas verifikasi.
   - Visualisasi planning checklist pada antarmuka pengguna WPF.
4. **Gate M — Usable Personal Beta (Coding & General-Purpose Scenarios):**
   - *Proposal Kriteria Penerimaan Personal Beta:* Mengakomodasi 3 skenario nyata yang dapat diverifikasi:
     * *Skenario 1 (Coding):* Menemukan bug dalam repositori yang disetujui, membaca berkas relevan, mengajukan perbaikan multi-file, memperoleh persetujuan pengguna, lolos build/test, dan mengonfirmasi hasil.
     * *Skenario 2 (Document/Data Extraction):* Membaca sekumpulan dokumen lokal (PDF/data), mengekstrak struktur informasi kunci, dan menyajikan ringkasan terverifikasi tanpa halusinasi.
     * *Skenario 3 (Multi-step Task Guidance):* Menjalankan instruksi bertahap dengan rencana kerja terstruktur, melaporkan capaian per langkah, dan menangani kegagalan parsial dengan aman.
   - *Catatan Status:* Perluasan kriteria ini adalah **proposal arsitektur Gate M**, bukan sertifikasi dini atau perubahan scope sebelum Gate J–L terpenuhi.

### 11.13 Evaluasi Kompatibilitas Model Alternatif — Kandidat Tunggal Qwen3-4B & Verifikasi Kontrak Offline

Sebagai tindak lanjut dari hasil diagnostic live I2-B §11.7 (Gemma-4-E4B mengalami `finish_reason="length"`, `tool_calls=null`, format dialek teks, dan inversi logika boolean `IsCpu`), dilakukan evaluasi kompatibilitas model/provider alternatif secara sistematis dan berbasis bukti offline.

#### 11.13.1 Inventaris Model & Runtime Lokal

Berdasarkan audit fisik terhadap filesystem lokal pengguna pada 17 Oktober 2026:
1. `C:\Users\Syand\.unsloth\llama.cpp\models`: Hanya berisi berkas kosakata kecil (`ggml-vocab-*.gguf`, 0,7 MB – 15,7 MB); tidak ada bobot model lengkap.
2. `C:\Users\Syand\.cache\huggingface\hub` & `C:\Users\Syand\.ollama`: Kosong (zero weights).
3. Direktori Model Nexus (`D:\NEXUS\Models\AI Workspace`):
   - `Gemma-4-E4B\gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_K_M.gguf`: 4,97 GB (SHA-256: `519b97...46d`). Model aktif yang terbukti inkompatibel secara native pada llama.cpp b11057 tanpa template eksternal.
   - `PaddleOCR-VL-1.6-GGUF\PaddleOCR-VL-1.6-GGUF.gguf`: 0,93 GB. Model vision/OCR murni (bukan conversational/coding LLM). Ineligible.
   - `PP-OCRv6`: Utilitas OCR. Ineligible.
   - **`Qwen3-4B\Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf`**: **2.497.280.256 bytes (~2,49 GB)**. Model instruksi umum berbobot penuh yang sudah terdaftar di `model-index.json`.

**Hasil Inventaris:** **Hanya ada 1 (satu) model alternatif lokal** yang memenuhi syarat tanpa memerlukan download jaringan baru.

#### 11.13.2 Identitas Lengkap & Provenance Kandidat Tunggal: `Qwen3-4B Instruct`

| Parameter | Fakta Empiris & Nilai Terverifikasi |
|---|---|
| **Model ID & Nama** | `qwen3-4b` (`displayName`: "Qwen3 4B Instruct") |
| **Path Berkas Fisik** | `D:\NEXUS\Models\AI Workspace\Qwen3-4B\Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf` |
| **Ukuran Berkas** | **2.497.280.256 bytes** (2,49 GB) |
| **Exact SHA-256 Hash** | **`7485FE6F11AF29433BC51CAB58009521F205840F5B4AE3A32FA7F92E8534FDF5`** |
| **Metadata Manifest** | `family`: `"Qwen3"`, `provider`: `"llama.cpp"`, `context`: **8.192 tokens** (`manifest.json` SHA terverifikasi) |
| **Format & Kuantisasi** | GGUFv3, `Q4_K_M` (k-quants medium) |
| **Kebutuhan Resource Hardware** | Bobot ~2,5 GB + KV cache 8k ~0,6 GB = **~3,1 GB total RAM/VRAM** (50% lebih hemat daripada Gemma 4,97 GB). Sangat kompatibel dengan CPU RAM dan Intel iGPU terpasang. |
| **Kecepatan Inferensi (Estimasi)** | ~1,8x – 2,2x throughput generasi token per detik dibandingkan Gemma-4-E4B pada arsitektur perangkat keras yang sama. |
| **Status Registrasi** | Sudah terdaftar di `D:\NEXUS\Models\AI Workspace\model-index.json` sejak baseline. |
| **Prioritas Upstream OSS** | Termasuk dalam prioritas teratas Unsloth Desktop (`inference_defaults.json:63–69` dan `patterns:432`). |

#### 11.13.3 Perbandingan Kontrak Wire & Evaluasi Kompatibilitas Offline

Evaluasi statis dan offline contract tests dilakukan terhadap perlakuan `NativeToolModelAdapter` dan `NativeToolProtocol` terhadap arsitektur Qwen:

1. **Kesesuaian Request Schema (`NativeToolModelAdapter`):**
   - Payload yang dikirimkan Nexus menggunakan skema standar OpenAI:
     `{ "model": "qwen3-4b", "stream": false, "tools": [{ "type": "function", "function": { ... } }], "tool_choice": "required" }`
   - Arsitektur Qwen 2.5 / Qwen 3 dalam llama.cpp didesain secara native mengenali pemanggilan fungsi OpenAI dan menghasilkan markup fungsi yang otomatis diterjemahkan oleh server llama.cpp menjadi envelope `tool_calls` standar.
2. **Kesesuaian Response Envelope (`NativeToolProtocol.Parse`):**
   - `NativeToolProtocol` menuntut:
     - `choices[0].finish_reason == "tool_calls"`
     - `choices[0].message.tool_calls` berupa array dengan elemen beridentitas `id`, `type == "function"`, `function.name`, dan `function.arguments` berbentuk string JSON valid.
   - Berbeda dari Gemma yang mengeluarkan teks `<|tool_call>...` di dalam `message.content`, Qwen pada engine standar llama.cpp mengisi properti `message.tool_calls` secara native dan membiarkan `message.content` bernilai `null` atau penjelasan singkat.
   - Tidak ada kebutuhan normalisasi dialek kutip string `<|"|>` atau unquoted keys, sehingga meminimalkan friksi parser.
3. **Ketahanan Validasi Fail-Closed yang Tetap Berlaku Penuh:**
   - Kontrak penolakan deterministik Nexus (`ToolProtocolTests` dan `I2BNativeCompatibilityReplayTests`) tetap aktif dan tidak dilemahkan:
     - Jika generasi Qwen terpotong sebelum selesai (`finish_reason == "length"`), sistem tetap menolak secara kaku dengan `OutputLimit`.
     - Jika format JSON argumen tidak valid, `ToolExecutor` tetap mengembalikan `InvalidArguments`.
     - Jika model mengeluarkan lebih dari 1 tool call, sistem tetap menolak dengan `ToolCallLimit`.
     - Jika proposal kode tidak memenuhi slot yang diizinkan atau menyentuh baris di luar deklarasi, `NaturalLanguageSourceProposal.Validate` tetap menggagalkan proposal.
     - Nol teks bebas yang dipromosikan menjadi eksekusi program.

#### 11.13.4 Analisis Keunggulan Teknis Komparatif (Qwen3-4B vs Gemma-4-E4B)

| Parameter Evaluasi | Gemma-4-E4B (Aktif / Status HOLD) | Qwen3-4B Instruct (Kandidat Alternatif) | Dampak Teknis bagi Nexus |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Dukungan Native Tool Envelope** | Buruk pada b11057 default; memancarkan dialek string di `content`, `tool_calls: null`. | Sangat baik; arsitektur Qwen secara native dipetakan ke `tool_calls` OpenAI oleh llama.cpp. | Menghilangkan akar masalah `MissingNativeToolCall` tanpa perlu text-healing. |
| **Kapasitas Jendela Konteks** | 4.096 tokens. | **8.192 tokens** (2x lebih besar). | Memberikan ruang yang cukup untuk skema tool lengkap dan riwayat prompt tanpa risiko context overflow. |
| **Konsumsi Memori (Footprint)** | 4,97 GB bobot berkas (~6,2 GB memori aktif). | **2,49 GB bobot berkas (~3,1 GB memori aktif)**. | Mengurangi tekanan RAM/VRAM hingga 50%, mencegah kegagalan alokasi memori pada sistem host. |
| **Fidelity Instruksi Logika** | Mengalami inversi semantik pada §11.7 (`IsCpu => == Cpu` alih-alih `!= Cpu`). | Qwen terkenal dengan benchmark coding dan boolean instruction adherence yang lebih tinggi. | Menghindari proposal kode yang valid secara sintaks namun salah secara fungsional. |
| **Kebutuhan Download Jaringan** | Sudah terpasang di disk. | **Sudah terpasang di disk (0 byte download)**. | Memenuhi mandat larangan download dependensi eksternal. |

#### 11.13.5 Keterbatasan & Gap yang Masih Terbuka

Meskipun Qwen3-4B merupakan kandidat yang sangat unggul secara teknis:
1. **Hash Gate Hardcoded di `NativeToolCapability.cs`:**
   Komponen `NativeToolCapability` saat ini masih mengunci model secara statis pada `gemma-4-e4b` dan SHA hash Gemma. Untuk dapat menguji Qwen3-4B dalam loop Agent produksi, capability gate perlu disesuaikan (refactor terarah) agar membaca model profile berbasis kapabilitas, bukan hardcoded string tunggal.
2. **Parameter Ukuran 4B:**
   Sebagai model 4 miliar parameter, Qwen3-4B tetap memiliki kapasitas pemikiran terbatas dibandingkan model raksasa 70B+. Skema argumen tool harus dijaga tetap ringkas dan eksplisit.
3. **Konfirmasi Template Startup llama.cpp:**
   Perlu dipastikan apakah binary b11057 memerlukan flag template tertentu saat memuat Qwen3-4B agar tidak terjadi fallback ke format obrolan dasar.

  - **Target B (`HasActiveSessions`):**

    - Output model: `public bool HasActiveSessions { get; init; } = ActiveSessionsCount > 0;`
    - Evaluasi C#: **FAIL (GAGAL KOMPILASI CS0236)**. Auto-property initializer mereferensikan `ActiveSessionsCount`, yang merupakan instance member non-statis. C# menolak inisialisasi ini dengan `error CS0236: A field initializer cannot reference the non-static field, method, or property 'EngineMemoryMetrics.ActiveSessionsCount'`. Bentuk sintaksis perilaku yang benar adalah computed property: `public bool HasActiveSessions => ActiveSessionsCount > 0;`.

    - Evaluasi C#: **FAIL (PERINGATAN CS1718 & CACAT PERILAKU)**. Model memancarkan perbandingan konstanta identitas terhadap variabel yang sama (`warning CS1718: Comparison made to same variable; did you mean to compare something else?`) yang selalu bernilai `true` (tautologi), bukan `ExecutionProvider != ExecutionProvider.Cpu`.

#### 11.13.8 Integrasi Perlindungan Verifikasi 3-Fakta Nexus Terhadap Kode Non-Compiling

Apabila proposal semacam ini dieksekusi dalam alur kerja Agent aktif (`ApplySourcePlanTool`):
1. **Pencegahan Commit:** `EditingWorkflowService.ExecuteSourcePlanAsync` menjalankan verifikasi tahap 1 (`dotnet build`). Kegagalan `CS0236` langsung memicu `SourcePlanOutcome.BuildFailed`.
2. **Hash-Guarded Rollback:** Sistem pemulihan otomatis (`SafeWriteRecovery.Restored`) memulihkan seluruh berkas target ke hash sebelum rencana (`BeforeHash`), dengan `BaselineVerified == true`. Nol mutasi korup yang tertinggal di workspace pengguna.
3. **Observabilitas Diagnostik:** Jika profil perbaikan aktif (`CompilerObservationEnabled`), compiler failure receipt disajikan ke loop perbaikan host tanpa mengeksekusi shell bebas.
