### 11.14 REA-Guided Model Capability Routing & Implementasi Qwen Seam

Sesuai persetujuan owner pada 17 Oktober 2026, dilakukan investigasi READ-ONLY terarah dengan REA 6.5.0 terhadap 4 aplikasi AI Agent referensi (Unsloth Desktop, OpenCode, DeepSeek Harness, Codex) untuk meneliti penanganan model capability routing sebelum mengimplementasikan perubahan minimal pada Nexus.

#### 11.14.1 Hasil Investigasi REA & Klasifikasi Bukti OSS

| Aplikasi Referensi | Komponen / Modul yang Diperiksa | Klasifikasi Bukti | Temuan Kunci Arsitektur & Perilaku |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Unsloth Desktop** | `template_capabilities.py`<br>`local_model_resolver.py`<br>`llama_admission.py`<br>`tool_call_parser.py` | **SOURCE VERIFIED** (Python AST / Upstream exact match)<br>**REA STATIC VERIFIED** (`llama-server.exe` b11160 PE resources `ev_9838ca3d2cfedfae448382c294edecb7bedfefd6cbeade08310d4762fde72a41`) | - `detect_tool_call_support` memeriksa template Jinja secara statis dengan `@lru_cache(maxsize=128)` dan fallback fail-closed.<br>- `local_model_resolver.py` memetakan GGUF lokal ke entri `_LocalGgufEntry` dengan meng-cache `mtime_ns` dan `size_bytes` untuk menghindari I/O disk berulang.<br>- `llama_admission.py` memarkir antrean inferensi saat dialog approval pengguna aktif untuk melepas slot execution.<br>- `tool_call_parser.py` menegaskan bahwa untuk model Qwen pada llama-server dengan template Jinja, pemanggilan fungsi dipancarkan natively sebagai OpenAI structured JSON tanpa memerlukan parser teks eksternal. |
| **OpenCode** | `packages/opencode/src/provider/models.ts`<br>`packages/opencode/src/provider/transform.ts` | **SOURCE VERIFIED** (TypeScript core)<br>**REA STATIC VERIFIED** (ASAR inventory `ev_0c130fe700943f7eaefdf25bab89752a96c5c1ba2cf353cc5c5fdddfadababee`) | - Model mendeklarasikan kapabilitas secara eksplisit dalam `ProviderCapabilities`: `toolcall: boolean`, `reasoning: boolean`, `input: modalities`.<br>- Pemisahan model chat biasa vs model tool dilakukan pada layer transformasi permintaan (`transform.ts`): tool schemas hanya dilampirkan jika `model.capabilities.toolcall === true`.<br>- Pergantian model (*model switch*) tidak merusak riwayat percakapan: pesan lama dipertahankan, namun kapabilitas tool dievaluasi ulang per giliran (*per-turn*). |
| **DeepSeek Harness** | `dsh/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/.../lib/index.js`<br>Tool Registry & Context Manager | **REA STATIC VERIFIED** (ASAR Analysis `ev_0fb0d7616fa3608a3c2590717fc4b8b32b27b440647e43ec59e244088643db32`) | - Tool registry terpusat dengan batasan eksekusi yang dikontrol host.<br>- Pendaftaran tool tidak memberikan hak eksekusi otomatis; eksekusi tool wajib divalidasi terhadap permission context.<br>- Jika model tidak mendukung tool yang diminta, orchestrator menolak pemanggilan tanpa fallback ke eksekusi arbitrary. |
| **Codex** | `core/src/model_info.rs`<br>`core/src/spec_plan.rs` | **SOURCE VERIFIED** (Rust Core) | - Pemisahan eksplisit antara model capabilities dan host execution authority.<br>- `ModelInfo.tool_mode`: `ToolMode::Direct`, `ToolMode::CodeMode`, `ToolMode::CodeModeOnly`, `ToolMode::None`.<br>- `ToolRouter` dibentuk per-turn berdasarkan `effective_tool_mode`.<br>- Pemaparan definisi tool (`EXPOSURE_DIRECT`, `EXPOSURE_HIDDEN`, `EXPOSURE_DEFERRED`) dipisahkan secara tegas dari hak eksekusi. |

*Batasan Otoritas Bukti:*
- **Zero Live Generation / Zero Runtime Observation:** Tidak ada inferensi baru atau observasi runtime dinamis yang dijalankan pada aplikasi referensi selama audit ini.
- **Pembedaan Tegas:** Seluruh temuan diklasifikasikan sebagai SOURCE VERIFIED atau REA STATIC VERIFIED; tidak ada klaim kehandalan runtime dinamis yang dibuat semata-mata dari keberadaan kode sumber.

#### 11.14.2 Perbandingan dengan Nexus & Keputusan Engineering

| Aspek Desain | Desain Referensi OSS | Implementasi Nexus Existing | Keputusan Nexus | Rationale Engineering |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Capability Seam** | Registry tabel profil / flags kapabilitas (OpenCode, Unsloth, Codex) | Hardcoded single check: `model.ModelId != "gemma-4-e4b"` | **ADAPT** | Ganti pengecekan tunggal dengan tabel profil kapabilitas terverifikasi (`SupportedProfiles`) yang mencakup `gemma-4-e4b` dan `qwen3-4b`. |
| **Multi-Model Caching** | Cache per-model berbasis path + mtime + size (Unsloth `_LocalGgufEntry`) | Cache skalar tunggal (`_identity` & `_support`), tertimpa saat model berganti | **ADAPT** | Ganti cache skalar dengan `Dictionary<string, (string Identity, NativeToolSupport Support)>` berdasar `ModelId`. Pergantian model tidak menyebabkan re-hashing file multi-gigabyte. |
| **Integrity & Trust Boundary** | Cek ekstensi / nama / mtime (Unsloth) | SHA-256 streaming verification untuk model GGUF dan engine biner b11057 | **KEEP NEXUS** | Pertahankan verifikasi SHA-256 kriptografis. Metadata filesystem (path/length/mtime) hanya digunakan untuk invalidasi cache, bukan pengganti verifikasi integritas isi berkas. |
| **Execution Authority Boundary** | Host permission prompt / approval gate (Codex, Harness) | Exact-plan one-shot approval + PlanId digest + 3-fact verification + SafeWrite | **KEEP NEXUS** | Pertahankan batas keamanan Nexus yang lebih ketat. Pendaftaran tool pada profil model tidak memberikan hak eksekusi otomatis. |
| **Admission Queue / Dynamic Loader** | Dynamic multi-slot queue, request parking (Unsloth) | Single-writer lease lock pada runtime terbagi | **DEFER** | YAGNI untuk penggunaan personal single-user saat ini. Menghindari penambahan kompleksitas dan layer framework kedua. |

#### 11.14.3 Implementasi Scoped C# pada `NativeToolCapability.cs`

Perubahan minimal diterapkan secara bedah pada [`NativeToolCapability.cs`](file:///D:/NEXUS/Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/Agent/NativeToolCapability.cs):

1. **Tabel Profil Terverifikasi (`SupportedProfiles`):**
   ```csharp
   private sealed record ModelProfile(string FileName, string ModelHash);

   private const string EngineHash = "C72F76994F8D680D7B423FF68662DE33857D2A2B001465DE55952873D4EF3DF3";

   private static readonly Dictionary<string, ModelProfile> SupportedProfiles = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
   {
       ["gemma-4-e4b"] = new("gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_K_M.gguf", "519B9793ED6CE0FF530F1B7C96E848E08E49E7AF4D57BB97F76215963A54146D"),
       ["qwen3-4b"] = new("Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf", "7485FE6F11AF29433BC51CAB58009521F205840F5B4AE3A32FA7F92E8534FDF5")
   };
   ```
2. **Multi-Model Independent Cache:**
   ```csharp
   private readonly Dictionary<string, (string Identity, NativeToolSupport Support)> _cache = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
   ```
3. **Penyaringan Fail-Closed:**
   - Model null atau kosong $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown`.
   - Model ID tidak terdaftar $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown` (tanpa membaca atau meng-hash berkas).
   - Nama berkas GGUF tidak cocok $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown` (tanpa meng-hash).
   - Direktori engine bukan `b11057` $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown`.
   - Berkas tidak ada di filesystem $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown`.
   - Hash model atau engine tidak cocok $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Unknown`.
   - Hash cocok $\rightarrow$ `NativeToolSupport.Supported`, disimpan di cache per model.

#### 11.15.1 Stage A — Audit Jalur Aktual Model Selection ke Agent & Bukti Model Switching

Audit jalur komprehensif dilakukan pada rantai pemanggilan produksi:  
`ConversationView (UI) -> ConversationViewModel -> ConversationRuntimeWorkflow -> SharedRuntimeAgent -> AgentModelSnapshot -> NativeToolCapability -> AgentOrchestrator -> NativeToolModelAdapter -> NativeToolProtocol -> Tool Registry -> ApplySourcePlanTool`

1. **Jalur UI & Model Catalog ke Agent:**
   - **UI Data Binding:** `ConversationView.xaml` membinding ComboBox model selector secara two-way ke `ConversationViewModel.SelectedModel`.
   - **Model Activation:** Saat pengguna memilih `Qwen3 4B Instruct`, `SwitchModelAsync` memanggil `ModelCatalogService.SetActiveModelAsync("qwen3-4b")` dan meneruskan lease runtime melalui `ConversationRuntimeWorkflow`.
   - **Agent Snapshot:** `AgentWorkspaceViewModel` mendelegasikan eksekusi Agent ke `SharedRuntimeAgent`, yang mengonstruksi `AgentModelSnapshot` dengan:

#### 11.15.2 Stage B — REA-Guided Compatibility Review (Unsloth, OpenCode, Harness, Codex)

Berdasarkan audit arsitektural REA terhadap 4 referensi OSS utama:
1. **Unsloth Desktop:** Model identity dan context lease dipisahkan antara background inference runtime dan UI orchestration. Diadopsi pada Nexus via `ConversationRuntimeWorkflow` dan `RuntimeConsumerLease`.
2. **OpenCode:** Provider capability routing dilakukan per-turn dengan fail-closed schema filtering (`NativeToolCapability.EvaluateSupport`). Tool schema hanya diinjeksikan jika model berstatus `Supported`.
3. **DeepSeek Harness:** Approval terikat pada snapshot rencana yang tidak dapat diubah (immutable plan digest) dan workspace boundary enforcement. Dipertahankan pada Nexus via `ApplySourcePlanArgs` validator.
4. **Codex:** Typed tool execution lifecycle dengan safe rollback dan verification boundary (build failure receipt). Diadaptasi pada Nexus via `ApplySourcePlanTool` dan `AgentSafeWriteTransaction`.

**Keputusan Arsitektur:** **ADAPT** pada boundary Nexus yang sudah ada. Tidak ada framework baru, adapter kedua, atau SafeWrite kedua yang dibuat.

#### 11.15.3 Stage C & D — Real Workflow Readiness, Disposable Fixture & Acceptance Oracle

1. **Disposable Fixture & Workspace Isolation:**
   - Fixture pengujian ditempatkan secara terisolasi pada direktori temporer (`Path.GetTempPath()`).
   - Sumber kontrak historis di `D:\NEXUS\I2B-ReviewedContracts` diverifikasi tetap 100% byte-identical (tidak pernah dimutasi secara langsung).
2. **Acceptance Oracle Terpisah dari Prompt:**
   - **Target A (`IsCpu`):**
     - Spesifikasi: Boolean `IsCpu` bernilai true jika execution provider **bukan CPU** (`provider != ExecutionProvider.Cpu`).
     - Oracle Truth Table membuktikan: CPU -> false; DirectML/Cuda/Vulkan -> true.
     - Menolak logika terbalik (`== ExecutionProvider.Cpu`) dan menolak tautologi `ExecutionProvider.Cpu == ExecutionProvider.Cpu` (output live sebelumnya).
   - **Target B (`HasActiveSessions`):**
     - Spesifikasi: Boolean `HasActiveSessions` bernilai true jika jumlah sesi aktif **lebih besar dari nol** (`ActiveSessionsCount > 0`).
     - Oracle Truth Table membuktikan: 0 -> false; 1/5/10 -> true.
     - Menolak `>= 0` dan menolak konstanta true.
3. **Pembuktian Kompiler Resmi (CS0236):**
   - Pengujian `CompilationProof_CompilerRejectsFieldInitializerReferencingInstanceMember_CS0236` mengeksekusi kompilasi riil melalui pinned .NET SDK 8.0.425:
     - Output model sebelumnya: `public bool HasActiveSessions { get; init; } = ActiveSessionsCount > 0;` terbukti **DITOLAK** oleh C# compiler dengan error `CS0236: A field initializer cannot reference the non-static field, method, or property 'ActiveSessionsCount'`.
     - Bentuk semantik yang benar: `public bool HasActiveSessions => ActiveSessionsCount > 0;` terbukti **PASS** dengan 0 error dan 0 warning.
4. **SafeWrite Rollback & Failure Receipt:**
   - `Workflow_SafeWriteAndRecovery_ProtectsDisposableWorkspaceOnFailure` membuktikan kegagalan kompilasi atau modifikasi tidak valid memicu rollback SafeWrite instan, memulihkan file baseline ke hash SHA-256 aslinya.

#### 11.15.5 Penutupan 4 Historical Regression Debts (§11.6)

Berdasarkan investigasi mendalam (Superpowers Systematic Debugging & TDD), keempat historical regression debts terbukti memiliki root cause nyata pada kode produksi dan test fixture, dan telah ditutup sepenuhnya dengan perbaikan minimal dan bedah:

1. **`GateIChatFirstWorkspaceTests.CF1_ExplicitCodeContextEntersChatPayload_QuestionTextNeverApproves_AndEditExcludesHistory` — CLOSED (PASS):**

IsReadOnly = true })`).
   - **Fix:** Menambahkan evaluasi prioritas: jika `Attachments.Any(a => a.IsReadOnly)`, pesan langsung diarahkan ke `ExecuteOrdinarySendAsync()` yang memformat `<lampiran>` ke dalam prompt chat tanpa memblokir izin baca disk.
2. **`ConversationViewModelTests.ConversationView_TopBarMenuButton_HidesInAiCheckMode` — CLOSED (PASS):**
   - **Root Cause:** Test fixture mencari tombol `BtnAIToggleSidebar` yang telah dipindahkan ke `DshSidebarView.xaml` (`BtnCollapseSidebar`) saat refactoring header C1.1. Tombol action bar atas aktual pada `ConversationView.xaml` adalah `BtnAITemporaryChat`, yang memiliki `DataTrigger` eksak `Binding="{Binding IsAiCheckModeActive}" Value="True"` dengan `Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Collapsed"`.
   - **Fix:** Memperbarui target query test ke `BtnAITemporaryChat` sesuai kontrak UI aktif bahwa tombol action bar atas kolaps saat mode AI Check aktif.
3. **`ConversationViewModelTests.I2A_ExplicitGrantInjectsActualSource_ExcludesHistoryAndAttachments_RevokeBlocksNextRequest` — CLOSED (PASS):**
   - **Root Cause:** Evaluasi `HasProjectReadGrant` ditempatkan setelah evaluasi lampiran umum, dan keyword array pada `NeedsProjectContext` tidak menyertakan kata `"read"` / `"baca"`, sehingga pertanyaan `"Read again"` setelah pencabutan grant tidak memicu status peringatan izin baca.
   - **Fix:** Menempatkan evaluasi `HasProjectReadGrant` di awal blok routing proyek aktif dan menambahkan `"read"` serta `"baca"` ke dalam keyword list.
4. **`NativeToolSelectionTests.BoundCompilerRepair_FiltersOtherDescriptors_AndFinalNoneStaysNone` — CLOSED (PASS):**
   - **Root Cause:** Ketidakcocokan pemisah direktori (path separator) antara event MSBuild (menggunakan Windows backslash `\`, misal `Tests\Tests.csproj`) dan konstanta profil `TestProjectRelative` (yang menggunakan forward slash `Tests/Tests.csproj`). Fungsi `Same` pada `CompilerArtifacts` melakukan perbandingan string ordinal ketat tanpa normalisasi slash, menyebabkan proyek tes tidak teridentifikasi (`Project(offending.Project) == null`), memicu cabang penolakan tugas compiler lain dan mengembalikan `process` tanpa menyegel `CompilerFailureReceipt`.
   - **Fix:** Memperbaiki metode `Same` pada `CompilerArtifacts.cs` agar menormalisasi separator (`a.Replace('/','\\') == b.Replace('/','\\')`) serta menormalisasi `TestProjectRelative` pada `SourceVerificationProfile.cs`.

### Empat historical fixes dan defect yang direproduksi

1. **Chat-first context — VERIFIED untuk regression/negative scope.** CF1 PASS pada final targeted run. Branch `Attachments.Any(IsReadOnly)` yang terlalu luas dapat menghindari prompt JIT pada pesan campuran attachment dan permintaan disk; tidak dibuktikan ada unauthorized disk read. RED membuktikan prompt hilang. Fix memakai supplied snapshot langsung untuk explicit explain action dan intent attachment-only yang sempit (attachment wording, tidak ada project/repository/disk/folder intent, file references harus cocok supplied labels). Attachment hanya supplied text, tidak menerbitkan grant. Mixed requests tetap melewati grant host; grant yang sah tetap mendahului route ordinary. History bukan source context; teks persetujuan bukan tool approval. Reviewer menemukan attachment-only regression pada fix awal; ditambahkan RED dan diperbaiki sebelum final PASS.

2. **I2A grant — VERIFIED untuk tested scope.** Historical grant/revoke test PASS. `Contains("read"/"baca")` menghasilkan false-positive bread/thread/pembacaan; tiga RED menjadi GREEN dengan word boundaries hanya pada dua kata tersebut. Read/Baca eksplisit tetap terdeteksi. Keywords mendeteksi intent, bukan authority. Existing host-issued grant, root/session binding, revoke dan mixed-message regressions berada pada impacted UI suite PASS. Tidak ada permission engine baru atau perluasan grant.

3. **Compiler repair — PARTIAL / execution BLOCKED.** Separator/case normalisasi Gemini dipertahankan setelah source review; exact target identity tidak menjadi prefix match. Lima pure identity cases PASS: separator/case accepted, traversal/unrelated root/suffix/null rejected. Source review menelusuri frozen project identity, expected failure receipt, SARIF diagnostic ID, descriptor filtering/final `none`, serta reverse recovery/hash checks. Historical bound compiler-repair test tetap FAIL pada verifikasi ini sebelum compiler receipt terbit (Build null), bukan ditandai PASS. Compiler-focused probe 1/20 PASS, 19 FAIL; jangan menafsirkan pure identity PASS sebagai certification build isolation atau recovery end-to-end.

4. **WPF AI Check — VERIFIED.** `BtnAITemporaryChat` adalah elemen current XAML yang relevan untuk top-bar temporary-chat action, bukan tombol obsolete. Historical contract test PASS; tambahan instantiated-view test memeriksa Visibility saat chat → AI Check → chat dan PASS. Tidak mengembalikan UI lama.

**Deadline defect — RED → GREEN.** `DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddSeconds(120)` adalah wall-clock deadline. Fake TimeProvider menunjukkan approval dapat diterima setelah elapsed expiry ketika jam mundur. Provider sekarang menyimpan initial remaining budget dan mengurangi elapsed monotonic; wall-clock expiry tetap additional fail-closed guard. Click dan post-await recheck tetap mengikat exact pending immutable plan/digest, cancellation/deny/revoke tidak menulis. Constructor publik tiga argumen dipertahankan; internal TimeProvider seam hanya untuk deterministic test.

UI menggunakan Stopwatch dan separate deadline CTS yang dilink ke execution/grant token, sehingga preflight juga masuk budget 120 detik; tidak menaikkan budget. Dua RED membuktikan deadline dan user cancellation saat preflight sebelumnya dilabeli Failed. Final UI membedakan TimedOut/ExecutionDeadline dengan Cancelled, menghapus approval availability setelah expiry, dan menampilkan remaining elapsed budget. Raw SharedRuntimeAgent proof untuk cancellation yang berasal dari outer UI masih dapat menyebut CallerCancelled; UI menambah `uiDeadlineExpired` tanpa memalsukan raw proof. Jika recovery melampaui deadline UI, status timeout dapat mengambil prioritas; recovery tetap perlu dinilai dari receipt sendiri.

Model HTTP generation cancellation, Agent run elapsed timeout, manual approval remaining time dan caller cancellation adalah boundary berbeda. Recovery bukan cancellation/deadline boundary yang sama: RecoverApprovedSafeWrite melakukan synchronous bounded-file rollback dengan CancellationToken.None; jumlah entries/bytes dibatasi tetapi tidak ada elapsed recovery deadline eksplisit. Owned-process cleanup punya fence tersendiri (5 detik), bukan timeout keseluruhan rollback. Ini SOURCE VERIFIED, belum merupakan reproduced filesystem-hang defect; tidak diubah tanpa recovery-policy review. Existing native run memakai stopwatch/timeout token; workflow admission wall-clock checks dipertahankan fail-closed. Tidak ada klaim seluruh deadline path telah runtime-certified. Separate **600s (10min) approval TTL**, measured monotonically from publication of the exact immutable validated diff.600s is a proposed usability choice, not an OSS-derived safety constant or a measured optimal value. Store it in reviewed host-profile policy, never model arguments or arbitrary prompt/UI timeout overrides. Other profiles/default Agent policies remain unchanged. Expiry denies and ends allocation; no republish/retry/refill. A later run needs a new allocation and fresh review.Use existing AwaitingApproval. Enter waiting once, only after the exact plan is frozen and no generation, process, other tool or mutation remains active. If quiescence cannot be established, deny instead of pausing. During waiting, no new model/tool/process/write/verification work; only manual decision, cancellation/revocation handling and inert display. Runtime and project lease invalidation/caller cancellation remain live and always dominate approval. Keep the current single-writer admission and verified dependency handles through the finite TTL; do not release/reacquire or weaken their authority in this delta. This extends dependency lock duration up to the longer review window and may block updates; disclose that tradeoff rather than silently redesign lease ownership. No source file should be written merely to hold a review.The existing run timing must become one host-owned **active accounting authority**, using TimeProvider timestamps/elapsed values through the existing services. Active remaining = initial180 - charged preflight/generation/tool/verification/finalization elapsed; exclude only this one counted review interval. Review TTL counts real monotonic elapsed, not polling increments. All outer workflow, SharedRuntimeAgent, source-tool and UI deadline consumers must observe the same transition and remaining allowance; old independent absolute CTSs must not keep ticking during review. Other tool descriptors retain their original bounds. On leaving wait, active time resumes from its previous remainder and includes revalidation; no fresh180s. UTC is receipt/display metadata for the new policy, not elapsed expiry authority; clock jumps in either direction must neither extend TTL nor incorrectly consume active time. Current A wall-forward fail-closed behavior is not silently changed before approval.After the Owner's exact decision, while resumption remains serialized: consume that run/plan/digest approval once, recheck caller/revocation, runtime/model generation identity, canonical root/path/reparse boundary, session, host revision, scope/read grant, profile/manifest digest, exact immutable plan digest, all before-source hashes and handle identities, verification graph/dependencies and positive active remainder. Existing bounded read authority must cover revalidation; exhaustion denies, never tops up. Reject on any mismatch without write. Recheck at the existing first-write and per-commit gates so drift after revalidation remains guarded. Only the existing host continuation resumes the suspended call; neither model text nor a new session may resume it. No extra tool dispatch/plan is charged or permitted because a human waited.Denial, TTL expiry, revoke, caller/runtime cancellation, stale/replayed/mismatched approval or lost session means no new mutation. An app/process restart abandons pending approval and active allocation, revokes grants and does not replay writes or auto-resume; retain diagnostic receipts only. Existing protected recovery remains awaited once a commit occurred; it may outlive active180s and review600s and must not be cut off by a timer. RecoveryConflict preserves external bytes and requires reconciliation. No claim of hard780s process lifetime, crash-safe rollback or persisted mutation replay is made. For an uninterrupted normal run with one waiting interval, accounted execution plus waiting is bounded by180+600=780s; scheduling/noncooperative drain/protected recovery are explicit limits of that accounting claim.**Minimal migration reach, only after approval:** update existing AgentRunProgress/AgentRunContext remaining-time contract; AgentWorkspaceWorkflow and SharedRuntimeAgent timer ownership; ToolExecutor source-plan timing; AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider TTL/serialized wait decision; ApplySourcePlanTool existing state/revalidation seam; AgentWorkspaceViewModel timers and separate active/review remaining display. ### 11.16.10 - REA-first architectural UX study: coherent Agent workspace (19 October 2026)

**DESIGN DRAFT / READY FOR OWNER DESIGN REVIEW. SOURCE VERIFIED and scoped REA VERIFIED; reference runtime UI NOT OBSERVED.** Owner requested whole-journey research/design, without implementation. Architectural Superpowers brainstorming applies; purpose and constraints are explicit. The finished wrap control is a narrow improvement, not product-wide redesign acceptance. `U = D:\NEXUS\scratch\ux-redesign-study-20261019`. This is the only Markdown update. No product source, configuration, dependency, candidate, approval policy or runtime changed; no build, tests, app launch or inference.

**Brief.** A personal Agent for coding and non-coding: retain conversation, task and context across request, activity, preview, proposed diff, manual decision and verified outcome. Make readiness, scope and the next action understandable without technical forms/JSON. Preserve native WPF, utilities, themes/accessibility and all existing host authority.

**A - Current UX assessment.** Exact ten-step map with source citations is in the ledger. Existing modern components are substantial:

| Current journey/component | Verified existing behavior | Opportunity |
|---|---|---|
| Launch/Home; App.xaml.cs:275-291, MainViewModel.cs:132, HomeView.xaml:13 | Catalog initialization, native shell, initial Home. | Home hero/activity card lacks direct new-chat/open-project/resume actions. Startup errors forwarded to Conversation may be away from the initial surface. |
| Navigation; MainWindow.xaml/.cs, DshSidebarView.xaml:209,335 | Global220/48 module navigation and Dsh history/search; existing mutual collapse. | Two information hierarchies; literal Ready footer is not runtime readiness. |
| Model; ConversationView.xaml:421, ConversationViewModel.cs:1491,1742, SettingsView.xaml:113 | Picker availability, serialized switch/rollback, import/scan/model badges. | Explain model selection, capability and readiness together; leave advanced management contextual. |
| Project/consent; ConversationView.xaml:994,1033,1123-1137, VM:1264-1278,2573 | Project chip, read grant/revoke, provenance and separate reviewed edit consent. | Consent discoverability. Direct-Edit/read-prompt detour is INFERRED from source, not a reproduced bug; test before any fix. |
| Chat; ConversationView.xaml:571,1347, VM:1281 | Selectable streaming markdown, attachments, history, copy/edit/regenerate and follow-tail. | KEEP; integrate Agent activity and review handoff into the task conversation. |
| Activity; ConversationView.xaml:532, AgentWorkspaceViewModel.cs:82 | Friendly status, changed files, remaining time, review/cancel and live announcement. | Summary lacks an ordered inspectable phase/tool history; some states fall back to generic processing. |
| Workspace; ConversationView.xaml.cs:101-116 |400px review when conversation width>=760, compact replacement below that, splitter and return to chat. | Wider/adaptive contextual work area. Hidden legacy RequestPane/tabs are NOT the current visible form: AgentWorkspaceView.xaml.cs:37-62 forces review-only mode. |
| Diff; AgentWorkspaceView.xaml:103-126/.cs:72-100 | File selection, colored lines, exact raw text, selectable code, completed wrap. | No full source editor, split view or hunk line-number metadata. Chat syntax coloring is not evidence of syntax-colored diffs. |
| Approval; AgentWorkspaceView.xaml:7,126,145; VM:148,259 | Manual exact-plan/digest commands, current CanDecide and separate pilot counters. | Stronger hierarchy and persistent review framing; preserve authority and timing. |
| Result; AgentWorkspaceViewModel.cs:77,140,229 | Typed build/test/final-verification success and honest recovery wording, raw receipts. | Structured passed/failed/not-run/unknown checklist and next action instead of mostly summary strings/technical detail. |

**C - Alternatives and recommended written design draft.**

| Option | Tradeoff | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| A. Conversation-led adaptive workspace | Conversation/task stays central; context, diff and results share identity and navigation. Fits existing VMs but needs coordinated presentation/lifecycle integration. | **RECOMMENDED** |
| B. IDE-first docking | Good for many source documents; adds floating windows/layout persistence and emphasizes coding. | DEFER until demonstrated multi-document/editor need. |
| C. Dashboard plus separate tool pages | Easy reuse of current module routes; leaves task context distributed across pages. | Keep utilities reachable, not primary Agent journey. |

A1. **Shell/navigation.** One project/conversation hierarchy with current tasks/needs-attention/recent work, plus Tools/Settings. Use existing metadata and current run/results; no new registry, persistent task engine or trajectory DB. Home offers new/resume/select actions and honest readiness. Unscoped chat stays available. Launch never resumes execution, grants or pending approval. Utilities for images/documents/files remain accessible; no parallel Agent promise.

A2. **Adaptive surfaces.** Proposed wide threshold1200 effective WPF DIP: navigation about220, chat min420, context/review min480 with existing bounded GridSplitter. Existing minimum900x600 stays. At900-1199, compact labeled/tooltipped rail, same list in flyout and one main Chat/Changes/Results surface. Preserve draft, reading position, selected file and task when only presentation changes. One authoritative VM/control instance, not duplicate request forms. Context/Changes/Results tabs reuse supplied or host-permitted documents, pending targets and typed results. Files means those known scoped items; general repository tree/search, terminal and unrestricted editor remain deferred/Gate J-K. Preview is read-only.

A3. **Conversation/activity.** Request -> host-observed phases/tools -> review -> application/verification -> result. Concise status plus expandable detail; routine groups collapse only outside keyboard focus, errors stay visible. Preserve existing follow-tail/return-to-latest. No invented percent/event/success or durable trajectory. Projection uses existing host facts; missing event exposure requires scoped planning review, not another executor. Cancel binds to exact run; show Stopping/Restoring until host drain/protected restore finishes.

A4. **Dedicated review.** AwaitingApproval opens Changes; wide view keeps chat alongside. Proposed/not-applied status, filenames/totals, compact file navigation and full-height unified diff. KEEP wrap/exact raw text. Reuse ColorCode only with exact-character/selection parity. Source line numbers require exact host range metadata; omit absent numbers. Optional split view later only for real need/width; no second diff engine. Large preview limits must be honest and never create approval of incomplete review. One action footer outside document scrolling: primary explicit Setujui perubahan, accessible Tolak, status/time and technical identity disclosure. Same plan/provider/CanDecide; no duplicate UI decision, chat-text approval, global Enter approval or default destructive focus.

A5. **Authority/timing/lifecycle.** Approved pilot only:180s active plus600s one-shot review, host-derived remainder, no UI authority timer/refill. Other profiles unchanged. Hide/tab/resize/theme changes never renew TTL/grant. Expired/revoked/stale/denied/cancelled decisions fail closed; host revalidation remains. Independent read-grant expiry can precede review TTL. MainViewModel.TransitionLifecycleAsync:321-338 calls module departure; ConversationViewModel.cs:1073-1076 cancels/drains Agent. KEEP this policy: internal surfaces stay inside the same workspace and never invoke module navigation. Project/session switching retains cancel/drain/revoke and fresh context. Utility departure retains the existing stay/cancel flow. Background continuation or detached approvals require a separate policy decision and are excluded.

A6. **Readiness/results.** Compact selected/loading/ready/busy/unavailable/error display from existing model/runtime/project facts; model name is not readiness. Required existing read/edit consents are visible near task entry, technical settings contextual. Reproduce suspected direct-Edit consent friction before exposing an existing host grant action. Results show Build/Tests/Final verification as Passed/Failed/Not run/Unknown and recovery as Restored/Conflict/Pending. Never use model prose as success. Logs/receipts remain accessible.

A7. **Visual/accessibility.** KEEP primitive/semantic/metric resources, icons and Light/Dark/HC. Use pane separators, spacing and type hierarchy instead of many nested cards; suggested14DIP body/12caption/18task heading, prefer existing tokens. HC uses system colors plus text/+/-; visible focus, native Tab/arrows, stable focus restoration and one principal scroll owner. Commands centrally conflict-checked and focus/IME aware (proposed Ctrl+L composer, Ctrl+Shift+R review). Keep native caption/snap; no replacement theme/window manager.

**Annotated concepts, DESIGN ONLY:** `U/concept-wide.png` (Dark wide), `U/concept-compact.png` (Light narrow). Both explicitly labeled draft/not implemented, with fictional files and synthetic timers. They are wireframes created after source research, not screenshots or Agent/test results. `U/draw-wireframes.py` draws artifacts only using an already bundled image library; no dependency installation. HC/native per-monitor DPI verification is future work, not proved by drawings.

**D - Reuse/dependency plan.** KEEP/ADAPT MainWindow/MainVM, Conversation/VM, DshSidebar, AgentWorkspace/VM, existing Grid/GridSplitter/TabControl/ListBox, RichTextBox/FlowDocument, markdown and ColorCode.Core2.0.15. KEEP/UPGRADE existing tokens, SettingsCard/InfoBar/Snackbar/ContentDialog presentation. No proven redundant component removed; deletion needs consumer inventory/migration and focused verification. Production PackageReference search found no Wpf.Ui/AvalonDock/AvalonEdit; adaptation comments are not installed-package proof.

| Library | Evaluation/decision, no install |
|---|---|
| Wpf.Ui | **DEFER package**, reuse compatible concepts. Official source targets net8.0-windows, MIT license. Existing house controls already cover shell; full package overlaps themes/resources and brings font/abstraction footprint. https://github.com/lepoco/wpfui/blob/main/src/Wpf.Ui/Wpf.Ui.csproj ; https://github.com/lepoco/wpfui/blob/main/LICENSE |
| AvalonDock | **DEFER**: fixed adaptive panes do not need floating windows or serializers. Root license Ms-PL, not assumed MIT; pin-specific notices required. Official migration docs state net8 re-added after5.0.2. https://github.com/Dirkster99/AvalonDock/blob/master/LICENSE ; https://github.com/Dirkster99/AvalonDock/blob/master/docs/migration/breaking-changes.md |
| AvalonEdit | **DEFER adoption**, preferred candidate if an actual editor gap is approved. Current source targets net8.0-windows and declares MIT. Editor is not a diff/authority engine. https://github.com/icsharpcode/AvalonEdit/blob/master/ICSharpCode.AvalonEdit/ICSharpCode.AvalonEdit.csproj |

These are current primary-source license/TFM observations, not selected release/maintenance/performance certifications. Footprints were NOT BENCHMARKED; no package compatibility build. Pin/review a release and measure candidate resource impact only if adoption becomes justified.

**B — Recommended written design: compact Chat entry + closable review tabs in the existing host.** Three presentation alternatives were considered: inline expanded diffs (OpenCode-like; can lengthen the conversation), side review tabs (Codex-like; reuses existing Nexus host), and both selectable modes (adds unnecessary duplicated interaction/state). Recommend side tabs with a compact file list in the current Chat task summary. This preserves Chat while opening detailed review on demand; no new preference/mode or shell navigation.

1. **Proposal:** the existing task summary lists affected files and actual +/- counts; diff content remains hidden until a file is clicked. No automatic takeover when a proposal arrives. This is largely existing behavior, not a claimed newly invented capability.
2. **Click/open:** select that exact current-plan target and open a read-only unified diff tab beside Chat. Tabs represent only opened files; repeated clicks focus the same tab. Opening a second file adds/selects its tab. Available targets remain restricted to current validated plan files; no repository explorer or disk read is added.
3. **Close/return:** tab × selects the nearest remaining tab; closing the last hides detail and returns focus to the invoking file entry/composer. Panel × uses existing HideReview semantics, never the cancelling CloseCommand. Closing/hiding does not approve, reject, cancel, revoke or extend clocks. Reopen retains file/wrap/scroll context for the same plan. Root/session/revision/plan replacement invalidates stale actionable state. Open tabs are a display subset, never a reduced mutation or approval scope.
4. **Readable diff:** compact file tabs, current target/path, consistent typography, +/- marker rail, syntax colors and themed added/deleted rows; one main diff scrolling surface with persistent footer. Unified baseline; existing wrap ON and horizontal scrolling OFF preserved. Previous/next file navigation uses actual plan entries; changed-block navigation only where supported by actual existing row data. No invented hunks, source context or source line numbers.
5. **Line numbers:** current ReviewDiffLine only carries kind/code/raw/newline, not old/new source coordinates. Original/new source numbers remain DEFERRED until a trustworthy typed mapping can be validated in a separately reviewed scope. Document row ordinals are not source numbers. No new diff engine, no rewritten diff text or digest, and no additional file reads to synthesize context. Mockups explicitly disclose unavailable source numbers.
6. **Approval:** actions remain outside the scroller, visually integrated with diff. Whole-plan file count stays visible even when a tab is closed. Use existing CanDecide, Approve/Reject and immutable plan/digest. Pilot active remaining and single600s review TTL shown separately, bound to host projection; panel close does not refill/pause either clock. After approval display pending host validation, then applied/verifying only on actual state; verified requires existing typed final receipt. Before-write deny/expiry is zero-write; cancellation after write must report actual recovery/outcome, never an unconditional zero-write slogan.
7. **Accessibility:** keyboard file/tab navigation and close scoped to focused review, no global Enter approval, no keyboard gesture silently accepting. Proper accessible target names, selected state, focus return, +/- labels in addition to color. Existing semantic Light/Dark tokens and SystemColors High Contrast retained. Existing compact mode replaces review surface within the same task and returns to same Chat; existing host breakpoint/splitter remain. Narrow mockups are conceptual, not native DPI certification.

**C — Mockups (design-only, synthetic data).** N contains eight independently drawn PNGs: `chat-closed-{light,dark}.png` for initial hidden detail; `wide-{light,dark}.png` after file clicks; `narrow-{light,dark}.png` using existing compact host; `panel-{light,dark}.png` closeups. All eight visually inspected for content/overlap. They are not edited reference screenshots, WPF renders or runtime evidence. Surrounding shell/Chat are placement illustrations only; they authorize no changes to those controls. Example review08:12/active02:07 are illustrative remaining values, not measured latency. Wide width depicts a user-adjusted existing splitter, not a proposed new default. Source metadata is never fabricated for visual parity.

**D — OSS decisions and migration cost.** KEEP current WPF host, RichTextBox, existing themes and installed ColorCode.Core2.0.15. ADAPT `NexusSyntaxColorizer.PopulateInlines` for diff Code separately from prefix; preserve exact code, spaces, newlines/raw copy and plain fallback. Per-line coloring has multiline-language-state limitations requiring focused rendering cases. Native marker rail first; no new general editor/docking framework.

AvalonEdit6.3.1.120 is MIT with native net8.0-windows7.0 package assets and no package dependencies ([package](https://www.nuget.org/packages/AvalonEdit), [tagged TextEditor source](https://github.com/icsharpcode/AvalonEdit/blob/v6.3.1/ICSharpCode.AvalonEdit/TextEditor.cs)). Conditional REPLACE/DEFER: useful for an editor-grade fixed margin if Owner requires it, but medium migration cost (trusted-row adapter, marker-aware syntax, colors/margins, theme and accessibility); it is not a diff engine and builtin line numbers label its document, not original source. No performance/integration result claimed. DiffPlex.Wpf1.9.1 is Apache-2.0, not MIT. DEFER: unified/split support does not establish our selection/wrap/syntax requirements; inspected `InternalLinesViewer` is TextBlock/StackPanel based, full viewer can regenerate a diff, and exact current-source-to1.9.1 equivalence was not established. No dependency installed or source copied. No Wpf.Ui/AvalonDock evaluation repeated: no demonstrated gap in existing shell/layout.

**E — Design-level delivery slices and acceptance (not a writing-plan yet).**

Compact task entry and closable tabs; integrated footer/full-height diff composition. | `Views/AgentWorkspaceView.xaml`, `.xaml.cs`, `ViewModels/AgentWorkspaceViewModel.cs` (ReviewFileViewModel is embedded). Whole-plan target summary never becomes the open-tab subset. |

Syntax and navigation using existing rows and renderer. | Same review files; reuse `AIWorkspace/Rendering/NexusSyntaxColorizer.cs` without a second renderer/engine. | Raw diff and plan/digest unchanged; exact content/whitespace/newlines/Unicode and copy/selection retained; wrap ON/OFF/horizontal scroll; file navigation and real changed-block boundaries; no false source numbers; plain fallback. |

#### A — Working interaction and actual changes

Existing Chat summary and file entries remain. Proposal publication keeps details hidden. Clicking a current-plan file opens/selects one tab in the existing review host; repeated click does not duplicate it. Close inactive preserves the selected file; close active selects a neighboring open tab; close last hides review and returns focus. “Tinjau perubahan” reopens the current/first file. Open tabs remain presentation state: closing them cannot narrow the complete immutable plan, deny/approve/cancel, or reset either clock. Foreign/stale objects and replaced plan/context cannot reactivate old tabs.

Unified rendering reuses the existing validated rows, native RichTextBox and installed ColorCode. Wrap defaults ON; OFF restores horizontal scrolling; each current-plan file retains its wrap/reading position. Existing raw diff, whitespace/Unicode/newlines, selection/copy and full plan remain intact. File navigation is bounded; change navigation visits contiguous changed blocks already present in row data. No invented source numbers, source context, diff parser, editor or filesystem authority.

The footer shows whole-plan scope and host-owned remaining time, with existing manual Approve/Reject commands. Approved means awaiting host verification, never already applied. Expired/stale rejection refreshes disabled presentation; successful verification still requires the existing typed receipts. Closing/reopening cannot rearm an expired/cancelled review. Enter in the diff does not approve; Approve is not a default key button and Reject is not a Cancel-key button. Focus returns to a still-valid originating file entry, otherwise existing composer. **Root cause CONFIRMED by deterministic reproduction.** Original operator publication created `owner-decision.json` with FileMode.CreateNew/FileShare.None, wrote/flushed while the final filename was already visible, then closed the handle. Driver.Publish's asynchronous reader polled File.Exists and called File.ReadAllBytes before that close. The share contract denies the read: IOException, reproduced HResult **0x80070020 / ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION**. Catch called provider.Deny("ManualDecisionInvalid"); it never completed normal Decide(false). Original pilot logged only Message, so its full stack/HResult were not recorded; the offline reproduction captures the corresponding CreateFile→ReadAllBytes→Consume stack, not an invented historical stack. Producer and consumer are the trusted local engineering operator/host, not model tools. GUID staging/final files are owned test artifacts; no Windows ACL/policy change or broader workspace permission.

**The two flows are distinct:** production WPF RejectCommand→AgentWorkspaceViewModel.Decide(false)→existing approvalProvider.Decide→pending plan continuation→terminal workflow. Production WPF has no decision-file bridge. Pilot operator Reject→decision publication→driver consumption→existing provider; the original run followed the error/default-deny branch. New offline evidence must not retroactively rewrite it as a normal UI Reject PASS.

**Minimal bridge fix — USE .NET atomic same-directory File.Move, KEEP provider authority.** F/DecisionBridge.cs writes a fresh GUID `.pending` file, flushes and closes it, then moves to the final decision name with overwritefalse. The final name is never published with an open writer; no loose FileShare.ReadWrite, transport retry, second approval engine or token/TTL change. Final JSON keys remain exact `runId/planId/planDigest/approved`, compatible with the original driver contract. A second publication cannot replace the existing file; pending leftovers are ignored and retained, not mass-cleaned. Matching/one-shot/expiry decisions still come from the existing provider. Read conflicts and invalid bindings retain default-deny. The consumed pilot driver/receipts are untouched; this tested publication implementation is for a separately prepared future driver/operator packet, not an automatic live restart.

**Bridge RED→GREEN:** forced overlap while the writer was held open produced12pass/1expectedfail (contention Reject), then atomic publication13/13.
